Therefore let me with all my earnestness pray at the lotus feet of my divine master to allow me to suffer the lot for which I am destined due to my past misdoings, but to let me have this power of recollection: that I am nothing but a tiny servant of the Almighty Absolute Godhead, realized through the unflinching mercy of my divine master. Let me therefore bow down at his lotus feet with all the humility at my command.

In 1961 you wrote an offering in which you strongly admonished your Godbrothers for neglecting to fulfill the orders of His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura.

…On this auspicious day I have come with this offering just to worship you, remembering your lotus feet.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s judgment is that renunciation is the most important thing. Not only that, but knowledge of such renunciation must be delivered to every living being.…

If one is bewildered by ignorance, what kind of renunciant can he be? He will only be a *phalgu-vairāgī,* renouncing externally.

Renunciation is actually the result of real spiritual emotion. Without such feeling, it is simply known as “show-bottle.”…

In performing dry renunciation, there can be no real preaching. Therefore *yukta-vairāgya* is given as the highest essential understanding.…

Generally the *sannyāsīs* renounce everything and go to stay in mountain caves, but you, O master, keep your *sannyāsīs* in mansions of marble.

To see a sense enjoyer is just like drinking poison, but you, O my master, go far and near, even abroad, to give them your *darśana*.

*Mlecchas* and *yavanas* are forbidden to enter the Hindu temples, but you, my lord, make them the chairmen and sit them in the assembly of devotees.

Hindus are not allowed to cross the ocean, but you send your devotees overseas to preach.…

The devotees want to hide in a secluded place to perform their *bhajana*. You, however, do not accept this in your judgment.…

To resolve all these apparently contradictory statements is not the play of some incompetent fool.

If everyone simply sat down together and considered these things, then what nice preaching there could be!

That is your order also, that everyone coming together should merge in your message and preach it to the world.

If everyone simply imitates, then there will only be a contradictory result. As long as it goes on, there will only be failure.

Even now, my Godbrothers, you return here in the order of our master, and together we engage in his *pūjā*.

But simply a festival of flowers and fruits does not constitute worship. The one who serves the message of the guru really worships him.

The service of the message is the real meaning of the *Vedas*. Don’t be proud, brothers. Come back to this.…

Oh, shame! My dear brothers, aren’t you embarrassed? In the manner of businessmen you increase your disciples.

Our spiritual master said to preach! Let the neophytes remain inside the temples and simply ring the bells.

All these things are not our spiritual master’s preaching methods. These are all the things done by the caste *gosvāmīs*.

But just take a good look at the terrible situation that has arisen. Everyone has become a sense enjoyer and has given up preaching.…

You have become renouncers, brothers, so renounce everything. But if you also renounce the order of your spiritual master, then what kind of renunciation is that?

The one who renounces his guru’s order (*guru-tyāgī*) and the one who tries to enjoy the assets of his spiritual master (*guru-bhogī*) are two kinds of useless persons. First become a servant of your spiritual master (*guru-sevī*), and then you will understand all these things clearly.

If there is only one Supreme Lord, then a true *sādhu,* even if his wealth increases, gives up his desire for profit, adoration, and prestige in this material world.

Your gold, brother, is the father of sense gratification. This meaningful statement was spoken by Prabhupāda himself.

Give up your wealth for preaching. Sit down together and make a special effort to come to a decision.

The Supreme Lord Himself says that everything is His. Don’t protest. Just unite and preach.

Śrīla Prabhupāda gave this final message himself. Take great care to do that completely and in all respects.

Otherwise your *sannyāsa* will be wasted and useless. O saintly ones, be careful; otherwise afterward you will regret.

So what is the difficulty for all of us to come together in this way? And why do all these things even have to be said to you?

Give up your stubbornness; there is no time! Come, all my brothers, on this auspicious holy occasion.

When will that day come when there will be a temple established in every house in every corner of the world,

When the high court judge will be a Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava with *tilaka* beautifully decorating his forehead,

When a Vaiṣṇava winning votes will be elected president of the land, and preaching will be spread everywhere?…

Wearing a two-*paisa* string, a man is called a *brāhmaṇa*. Wearing a saffron cloth, he has become a *sannyāsī*.

The householders beg from the *sannyāsīs,* and why not? The *sannyāsīs* have tens of millions of rupees in the bank.

As the days pass, the influence of Kali-yuga increases and the poor *jīvas* are all crying in distress.

Ten thousand cows are killed daily, and the nation’s expert leaders are all eating untouchable foodstuffs.

People of muddy intelligence increase day by day. A man and his wife separate simply on a mere word.

Everyone has become a witch by the influence of the age. Everyone is unhappy due to the absence of the mercy of the Vaiṣṇavas.

The Vaiṣṇavas are famous as *para-duḥkha-duḥkhiḥ*. This fame will increase as the preaching increases.

If eternally pure devotional service to Kṛṣṇa is awakened in everyone, then Kali will flee, screaming, of his own accord.

*Prāṇinām upakāraya:* do work for the benefit of all living beings. This is Mahāprabhu’s teaching. This is the storehouse of happiness in this life and the next.

So much work has fallen into your hands, so gather together and do it.…

The mercy of the Vaiṣṇavas is scientific and authorized. Without the Vaiṣṇavas’ mercy, this world is simply insurmountable *māyā*.

If there is a kingdom of Viṣṇu and Vaiṣṇavas on the surface of the earth, then the world will be happy. So say all the great sages and *ṛṣis*.…

Today, my brothers, get to this task. Save the battered souls by your preaching.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please be merciful today. This time have compassion. You are not a conditioned soul of this material world.

Whatever independence one may have, let it be cast into the waters. The lowly Abhaya offers this presentation today.

How relevant those words still are!

It would seem then that Vyāsa-pūjā offerings serve many purposes in addition to the glorification of the spiritual master—one of which is to rekindle the loyalty of all the disciples and followers to the order of their master. It is hoped that by highlighting a specific attribute of yours through this humble homage, we may draw everyone’s attention to an important legacy of yours that we must endeavor to keep alive.

You would sometimes say that it is not enough to argue that God is great; we must know *how* He is great. Similarly, this offering will attempt to highlight just one of the many ways in which you are great, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we can learn from your example and, as those entrusted with preserving and further developing your legacy, can ensure that we embody your example to the extent we are able.

There are many ways in which you can be considered or described as great, Śrīla Prabhupāda. One could highlight your achievements, your personal sacrifices, your dedication to your spiritual master—all of which are linked, of course. These are standard measures of greatness. But in your personal dealings we find some more elusive elements of greatness—the kind of things that people might tend to overlook (or not realize are measures of greatness). The interesting thing is that there is an aspect of greatness that all of us are capable of—and yet most of us miss.

It is in how one treats the “little people.” You always treated everyone as though their existence mattered to you. So-called great people (people with your kind of accomplishments) often don’t have much regard for “little people” (people who can do little for them).

You delighted in the small successes of others—sharing the excitement with the Agarwals when their son took his first steps (showing that he too had significance).

You demonstrated greatness of heart in allowing the Agarwals to continue with their bad habits (like eating meat) even in your presence without feeling ashamed.

People’s recollection many years later of the little Indian Swami who brought them *samosas* while selling books door-to-door in the early days in New York shows the impact your dealings made.

The time you spent explaining your vision for the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement to Mr. Ruben communicated to him the worth that you saw in him.

Your dealings with a “bum” who brought you some toilet roll for your first storefront temple reveal your unconditional appreciation for even the slightest amount of service that someone may manage to perform.

You never gave up on your Godbrothers, always inviting them to share in your success, thus showing your magnanimity.

You overlooked the craziness of your first followers, seeing past what they were at the time to see what they might become. You never looked down on anyone unless it was to help them up. You shared your meals with them. You waited in the queue for the bathroom. The qualities of patience and forbearance you displayed in those formative years of the movement were as essential to the movement’s development as your words.

You always made time for people—to answer their questions, to reply to their letters, etc. No one was made to feel small or unworthy.

You caught people doing things right—not focusing on their shortcomings—showing that encouragement grows the movement more than fault-finding.

Your loving dealings with baby Sarasvatī demonstrate how important the Society’s children are. Your dealings with children consistently demonstrated this.

You showed compassion to your female disciples and awarded them second initiation, breaking with tradition, to show them that you valued their commitment too.

You cried if the news reached you that even one disciple had left.

You handed over the running of the movement to your disciples to encourage their growth.

When someone publicly discussed the details of someone’s falldown, you scolded them because they had made it more difficult for the person to return. You were concerned that a person’s dignity be preserved.

You filled people with the sense that they could achieve success for Kṛṣṇa in their projects and in their lives. You ensured that everyone understood the value of what they were doing and participating in—and that their contribution was worthwhile.

The impact that you made in a few hours on a young Russian boy brought about a nation of devotees.

Once in Brooklyn you held open a door for a young mother and child while the devotees were performing *kīrtana* all around. Acts of charity (and good manners) are never to be given up.…

Your dealings with Hanumān Goswami show how valuable people are to you even if they blunder.

Your bringing back the devotees from Japan when you heard about their spiritual crises in spite of the money they were raising demonstrates your clarity about what really matters most.

When you saw the starving children in Māyāpur picking through the leftover plates, you impressed upon the devotees the importance of *prasādam* distribution.

You even let a race-car driver talk at length about his passion for driving to show that his story had worth too.

You asked your older disciples for their opinion to teach that others’ views had value.

Your asking what the devotees had for lunch *prasadam* even months into your fast in your final days shows your concern for your sons and daughters. *Catur-vidha-śrī-bhagavat-prasāda*…A spiritual master is more concerned that others are taking *prasādam* than with his own eating arrangements.

These are just a few examples.

Would that we could learn from your example what really matters—what true greatness is. It is not to do with size or impressive grandeur. Would you have achieved the things you did if you had treated people differently? Would ISKCON have become so prominent? Will it endure if we fail to grasp this simple truth and fashion our own hearts and characters to represent what you offered us? It is still primarily you who are attracting people to involve themselves in the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. And why? Because people feel that their service counts for something in your eyes—that they matter to you. Such is your greatness. Nothing compensates for genuine love for others. It is that spirit which grows the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Sometimes in our zeal to spread your mission I believe we may fail to grasp your spirit—that each and every person matters. “All of the effort to establish temples and print books will be worthwhile if even one person becomes a pure devotee.” But if we become overly absorbed in maintaining cashflows, filling *rotas,* making policy decisions, repairing buildings, even fulfilling spiritual obligations in a spirit of duty and loyalty, but we overlook and neglect people, then what has this to do with your mission? We will have missed the point—that’s the difficulty with simple truths.

We’re often concerned with what you said in a particular circumstance but fail to appreciate how you are—the value you placed on certain things (and people). If we get caught up in trying to please you with the grandeur of projects and tasks enacted in your name, but lose the spirit of compassion for living beings, then we will have missed the true essence of your mission.

Your servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor, UK.  
(written by Dāmodara dāsa)

## Bhaktivedanta Village (France)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you, your movement, and your devotees!

We are very glad to offer you our prayers on this auspicious occasion, and we want to give you our report as a sign of our appreciation. You sustain our devotion; although we are very weak in devotion and cannot claim to be your direct servants, we just want to be the servants of your servant. We know that by this service our contaminated consciousness will vanish and we will be able to relish *śravaṇam* and *kīrtanam* better. By service to the pure devotees, everything that troubles the heart will be reduced to nil.

As you may already know, we have 250 hectares of land, most of which is already producing grains, beans, and a wide variety of vegetables. We are now the leading farm in ISKCON in agricultural production. Our *gurukula* is setting up, and families are trying to establish their households here. Although we are facing difficulties in being accepted in this part of France—it’s hard to get permission from the government authorities to build, etc.—we are confident that if we are patient, compassionate, and well-behaved, the hearts of the local people will soften. Since our place is called Bhaktivedanta Village, we want to represent you properly. Then you will be glorified, along with Lord Caitanya and Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

We pray only to remain together to serve you better, to give everyone a lasting good impression of ISKCON.

Your tiny servants in Dole, France, at a tiny place called Bhaktivedanta Village.  
(written by Divya Puruṣa dāsa)

## Bhubaneswar

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*vāñchā-kalpa-tarubhyaś ca kṛpā-sindhubhya eva ca*  
*patitānāṁ pāvanebhyo vaiṣṇavebhyo namo namaḥ*

O dearest, most revered, most blessed, and most affectionate eternal truest spiritual grandfather and ever-glittering *sad-śikṣā-guru* Śrīla Abhaya Charanaravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, kindly accept our prostrated full-hearted *daṇḍavat praṇāmas* at the holy speck of your divine lotus feet again and again on this most auspicious occasion of your sacred divine appearance.

To mundane vision you may not be visible, but…

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die,  
When thou art living still in sound.  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living  
Try to spread the holy name around!

O dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, though we are all very contaminated here, still we truly feel in our heart of hearts the nectarean words of *parama-bhakta* Uddhava, which are absolutely applicable to Your Divine Grace as well:

*naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo ’ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caittya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features—externally as the ācārya and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.29.6).**

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, in addition to remaining in our hearts in the form of remembrance of your most perfect *ācārya-līlā,* conducted according to the higher transcendental arrangements of Kṛṣṇa in the form of the Supersoul, you also remain in our hearts in the form of your ever-ecstatic *vāṇī* to prompt us how to successfully march toward the supreme divine destination, i.e., the absolute divine lotus feet of the Supreme Divine Couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

Furthermore, we cannot attain devotional service without your divine grace, a fact confirmed by no less an authority than the Supreme Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself:

*mahat-kṛpā vinā kona karme ‘bhakti’ naya*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakti dūre rahu, saṁsāra nahe kṣaya*

**“Unless one is favored by a pure devotee, he cannot attain the platform of devotional service. To say nothing of** ***kṛṣṇa-bhakti,*** **one cannot even be relieved from the bondage of material existence” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya*** **22.51).**

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your own example have most perfectly demonstrated what a true *ācārya* should be.

*āpane ācare keha, nā kare pracāra*  
*pracāra karena keha, nā karena ācāra*  
*‘ācāra’, ‘pracāra’,—nāmera karaha ‘dui’ kārya*  
*tumi—sarva-guru, tumi jagatera ārya*

**“Some behave very well but do not preach the cult of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, whereas others preach but do not behave properly. You simultaneously perform both duties in relation to the holy name by your personal behavior and by your preaching. Therefore you are the spiritual master of the entire world, for you are the most advanced devotee in the world.”**

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, who, other than the blessed one empowered and energized by the unlimited supreme energy of the Supreme Absolute, Lord Kṛṣṇa, could do what you did?

*kali-kālera dharma—kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana*

**“The fundamental religious system in the Age of Kali is the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Unless empowered by Kṛṣṇa, one cannot propagate the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **movement” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Antya*** **7.11).**

Therefore, O Śrīla Prabhupāda, the holy offering meant for Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī is also perfectly applicable to Your Divine Grace.

*śrī-caitanya-mano-’bhīṣṭaṁ sthāpitaṁ yena bhū-tale*  
*svayaṁ rūpaḥ kadā mahyaṁ dadāti sva-padāntikam*

And now at the end of this offering, O dearest great master, let us recall the noble prediction Your Divine Grace once boldly expressed during the foundation-stone-laying ceremony of the temple of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma here at Bhubaneswar on the auspicious occasion of Lord Nityānanda’s holy appearance day, i.e. Nityānanda Trayodaśī, way back in 1977: “This temple of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma here at Bhubaneswar will one day become one of the best among all the Vaiṣṇava centers of the world.”

And, O great Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that Your Divine Grace put your heartfelt full faith in your dear, pure, and true disciple His Holiness Gaura Govinda Swami. But now, with his painful departure in 1996 from the visible worldly scene, we appeal at your divine lotus feet to please enable us to become your qualified grand-*śiṣyas*.

Your aspiring grand-disciples at your last-founded temple, the Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple in Bhubaneswar, India.

## Bogota

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

For the last few years I had doubts that my service here in Bogota, Colombia, had any value at all. I even neglected to write some small homage on your appearance days. Please forgive me for this negligence.

Yet now, by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy coming through Your Divine Grace, I am again sufficiently conscious of my duty and my innate desire to be able to pen these faltering words. Perhaps it is because things are finally starting to take shape here—after such a long struggle—that I feel fit to say something to you at this time.

Just as the myriad intimate relationships Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa enjoys with untold numbers of confidential devotees don’t in the least diminish the eternal fact of His majesty, so your tender loving care (including your loving chastisement) of so many fallen souls, your affectionate exchanges with us, in no way diminishes your unique standard of special empowerment, your awesome stature as Lord Caitanya’s very special emissary to this world.

Just yesterday you were here with us, or so it seems, but then again the years are indeed accumulating since that bleak November of your final manifested pastime of going back to the spiritual world to eternally relate with Kṛṣṇa in your unique intimate relationship with Him. But as your disciple Nirantara dāsa has sung, “Now that you’re gone, we carry on, and we love you…Prabhupāda…” Yes, most of us are still in these bodies in this world of *māyā*, but we are trying to catch the hint you left us, the instruction of the Lord: “Now that you’ve come to this miserable temporary world, just render loving service to Me.” And this means we must serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and serve and struggle within your ISKCON.

Here in Colombia, Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavism is manifest, an extension of your mercy, and yet it is a bit sad because your ISKCON shelter is generally avoided instead of embraced. We the faithful minority feel so transcendentally proud to have as our mission the upholding of your most glorious International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Never mind that “the names have been changed to protect the innocent” (or maybe to goad us to endeavor more and more for our own purification so Kṛṣṇa may one day soon return to us our proper designation). We have just a rented a new place, much more suitable than our previous location, a newly-arrived dynamic devotee from the Caribbean is enlivening us with his great preaching spirit (what to speak of his transcendental cooking!), your living presence in thousands of your books for *saṅkīrtana* is about to manifest here—so many blessings are being showered upon us, thrown from Lord Caitanya’s pinnacle and delivered by his representative, the special general in His army, Your Divine Grace.

Can I say a few personal words? Please let me ask you: Does it mean anything that we met in 1966? Even though I balked at your invitation for me to join up then and there, is it wrong for me to cling to the possibility that you remember me, that you haven’t written me off as a total nonsense? A few years later I reminded you of our encounters in your apartment in ’66 by telling you that then I was really, really crazy. You immediately raised your eyebrows and pointedly questioned, “Oh, and are you *still* crazy?” We sat down, and a brief meeting ensued. As I was about to take my leave, you uttered the last words you directly spoke to this fallen rascal: “…and we are not asking for any money!”

But I keep struggling to collect, not money, but *lakṣmī*, although you did make a special request, an intense entreaty, that I “simply try to understand Kṛṣṇa.”

You were magnificent and I was horrid. It can only be Lord Caitanya’s great mercy that you have allowed me to don my ragged orange *dhotīs* in your ISKCON movement all these years. I am so fallen—but I will never again walk away from you.

Please just glance one relishable second over this prostrated ex-hippy, this aspiring disciple, crying in the dust under the cooling shade of your divine lotus feet.

Your devotees in Bogota, Colombia.  
(written by Prabhupāda dāsa)

## Boise

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Lord Caitanya and to your incessant service for His *saṅkīrtana* movement. As we compose this Vyāsa-pūjā offering, we remember the last *śloka* in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (18.78):

*yatra yogeśvaraḥ kṛṣṇo yatra pārtho dhanur-dharaḥ*  
*tatra śrīr vijayo bhūtir dhruvā nītir matir mama*

**“Sañjaya said, ‘Wherever there is Krsna, the master of all mystics, and wherever there is Arjuna, the supreme archer, there will also certainly be opulence, victory, extraordinary power, and morality. That is my opinion.’”**

Your life and movement constitute a first-class example of the truth of this verse. Kṛṣṇa is certainly on your side as you fight one battle after another through your wonderful ISKCON. Like Arjuna, your movement takes both victory and loss in stride. Your sons, daughters, and grandchildren score one success after another, opening new majestic temples. The victory at Bhaktivedanta Manor that shook the British Parliament, the opening of the majestic temple in Bangalore, and the newly-built Glory of India temple and cultural center in New Delhi are just a few examples.

As I travel all over the world, I find a monument or two to so-called moralistic, powerful leaders here and there, but there is no spot in the world where you are not revered and worshiped.

Your extraordinary power and impact are reflected in the words of tribute paid by Indian Prime Minister Vajpayee to you and ISKCON at the inauguration of the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Pārthasārathi Temple and Glory of India center in Delhi:

The ISKCON movement has few parallels in the world in terms of its rapid global spread, its trans-national, trans-ethnic, and trans-professional appeal, its outward simplicity, and the devotional energy of its followers. In the less than three and half decades since its inception it has established temples in practically all parts of the world, and many of them are marvels of beauty, such as the one that is being opened in Delhi today.…

The *mahā-mantra* of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Rāma reverberates to the dancing feet of ISKCON devotees each morning and evening in temples from Stockholm to Sao Paulo and from Miami to Māyāpur. But what ISKCON has achieved is indeed globalization of the *Gītā* appeal. Some people say that my government is opposed to globalization, but let me say that I am all in favor of globalization of the message of the *Gītā*.…If today the *Bhagavad-gītā* is printed in millions of copies in scores of languages and distributed in all nooks and corners of the world, the credit for this great sacred service goes chiefly to ISKCON.…

For this accomplishment alone Indians should be eternally grateful to the devoted spiritual army of Swami Prabhupāda’s followers. The arrival of Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda in the United States in 1965 and the particular popularity of his movement gained in a very short span of twelve years must be regarded as one of the greatest spiritual events of the century.…

And all this in only the first thirty years of your movement! We cannot comprehend what the 9,970 years of your opulence, victory, and extraordinary power will bring for the benefit the world.

We only pray that you cast just a fleeting glance on us in Boise.

Your most humble, aspiring servants, in New Biharvana, in Boise, Idaho, USA.  
(written by Anantarūpa dāsa)

## Bologna

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance in the blessed dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you!

This blessed event of your Vyāsa-pūjā always helps me understand how deeply indebted I am to you. As Kali-yuga progresses very rapidly, by your mercy millions of books are being distributed to save millions of souls. There are not enough words to express my feelings of indebtedness for this mercy of yours. I can say boldly that you are the best personality in this creation, for you are giving so much so freely—so much love to everyone. Love is such an important spiritual element.

This pure sentiment of real love is very much missing in the material world. Yet even though love very much misunderstood by materialists, still for your devotees the same love in the pure form is the basic principle of spiritual life. My innermost desire is to develop more love for you, for the *saṅkīrtana* mission, for your devotees, for ISKCON, for chanting the *mahā-mantra,* etc. Again and again you said, “Please distribute my books.” In this simple and profound instruction is the *bīja* (seed) formula to increase more and more our pure love for you and Kṛṣṇa. This is your oceanic mercy, and for this I am very grateful.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda bless all the devotees of our preaching center.

All glories to your Śrī Kṛṣṇa *saṅkīrtana*! All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

The devotees of Bologna, Italy.  
(written by Durgama dāsa)

## Bosnia and Herzegovina

Our dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet! All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, what can we say? What can grandchildren say about the glories of their grandfather? We are not even aware what you have done for us, for the whole world, for this universe.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, if it were not for you, if the Lord had not sent you to this fallen planet, we would be still suffering in the most hellish conditions of life. We were drowning in the ocean of material existence, and you came with your boat to save us and take us back where we really belong—back home, back to Godhead. You gave us the chance to become civilized human beings, for we were living like animals and going straight to hell. You built a magnificent, perfect home for everyone—ISKCON—a home big enough and merciful enough to accept everyone. Actually, ISKCON is you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, because there cannot be a home without a host. So you are welcoming all conditioned living entities to your home and creating one family with your love for us. You gave us that warm, loving place we were all looking for. You left us your dear disciples, our spiritual fathers and uncles, who are leading us now.

With your arrival on this planet, a new dimension was created. The whole spiritual world was with you because, actually, you never left it. Being an exemplary pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa, you were always absorbed in spreading this wonderful Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in all the countries of the world, never fearing, always depending only on Kṛṣṇa’s mercy and boldly battling against *māyā*.

Five hundred years ago Lord Caitanya appeared on this planet and stated that *saṅkīrtana*, the chanting of His Holy Names, would spread to every town and village of the world. He promised that, but it took one *senapati* devotee to fulfill His promise. Just by following the order of your Guru Mahārāja, you fulfilled the promise of Lord Caitanya! With your every act, every word, and every step you showed us what to do, how to become first-class human beings, how to become Vaiṣṇavas. You taught us how to serve Lord Kṛṣṇa with devotion, for that is the only way to achieve love of Godhead. Always humble and completely surrendered all your life, you were the perfect example of *dāsānudāsa*. Verily, you are the embodiment of what the scriptures say about a pure devotee!

Serving your guru and Kṛṣṇa was your only thought, your life and soul. Just by following the footsteps of the previous *ācāryas,* you were able to do what nobody has ever done or will ever do in the future. Whenever you saw a person with even the slightest desire to serve Kṛṣṇa, you would fan it and fan it until it became a real fire of devotion, a fire to spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement.

So, what can we do, we people of Kali-yuga, with our dry hearts black as coal and dull heads devoid of intelligence? The only way for us to try to satisfy such a great personality as you is to push on this *saṅkīrtana* movement! To distribute books, or at least to help those who are distributing them, will make our lives perfect. You said, “Books are the basis.” But these books are you. You still live in them, and actually you never left us, for you are nondifferent from your words. Your “emotional ecstasies,” your purports to the verses of the scriptures, are our everyday *darśana* with you.

None of us here in Bosnia ever saw you. We met you through your books and your representatives, and thus you completely changed our lives. From *yavanas* we are trying to become Vaiṣṇavas. While we are trying to serve you and spread this wonderful *saṅkīrtana* movement, we must beg for your blessings, because without them our whole endeavor is nothing. Only with your mercy do things happen. May your glories be spread throughout all three worlds!

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your insignificant servants in Bosnia and Herzegovina.

## Boulder

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you and your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the universe!

In this small town among many towns and villages, we are attempting to give bring some conditioned souls to shelter of your lotus feet. So many philosophies, teachers, and yogis they have heard, but without Kṛṣṇa, their hopes for liberation look bleak.

“O lotus-eyed Lord, although nondevotees who accept severe austerities and penances to achieve the highest position may think themselves liberated, their intelligence is impure. They fall down from their position of imagined superiority because they have no regard for Your lotus feet” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 10.2.32).

An instrument must function,  
not just appeal to the eye.  
Otherwise the wise musician  
will simply pass it by.

O great orchestrator  
who fulfill the Lord’s desire!  
Please make a tune with our youth and minds  
to extinguish the blazing fire

Of material desires within our hearts,  
and make us so sincere  
that we represent you properly  
for eager souls to hear.

Praying to be the eternal servants of your sons and daughters in the *saṅkīrtana* mission, we remain

Your servants in Boulder, Colorado.  
(written by Madana-Gopāla dāsa and Pañca-Tattva-devī dāsī)

## Brisbane

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

The devotees of ISKCON Brisbane have been steadily trying to move forward this year in the fight against the material energy. By your mercy many new devotees have joined the temple, and they are enthusiastically serving your mission.

Now what we want to see is a unity among the devotees, not just in this temple but in all the temples all over the world. When we are all able to work without self-interest and put aside differences, then we can see the actual goal. Since your mission is nondifferent from that of Lord Caitanya, you will not be satisfied until everyone has become blessed by the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

By strong preaching and association with your advanced devotees, as well as by strict adherence to the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* and observance of the regulative principles, we will all become infused with the necessary potency to achieve that mission. This is true for everyone, whether one is a new devotee or an old-timer. The material energy is relentless and does not differentiate on the basis of past credits.

We need to connect with the spiritual potency and work on that platform. Mistaking passion for advancement, we have been led down the wrong path too many times. We therefore sincerely pray that you help us become worthy devotees and realize the true import of the meaning of what it is to live and breathe the actual life of a Vaiṣṇava.

Your servants at the ISKCON Brisbane, Australia, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Mandir.

## Cape Town

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Wherever we look, we find evidence of your merciful presence. In one way or another, persons are becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. At the university, on the streets, in the shops and businesses, at homes, in the squatter camps, many know and appreciate the devotees, the holy names, your books, *prasādam,* and the process of devotional service.

In contrast, Kali-yuga aspirations are widely advertised, influencing persons in highly sophisticated ways to dive headlong into sense gratification as the ultimate goal of life.

Yet even though we, as preachers, may not necessarily have the material skills to match those of persons promoting such gross material life, we see that others are attracted to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are amazed when we witness individuals abandoning their previous conceptions and attachments and offering themselves to Kṛṣṇa in one way or another: they give up their time and abundant opportunities for sense gratification to come to programs to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, hear about devotional service to Kṛṣṇa, and take Kṛṣṇa *prasādam;* they offer their *lakṣmī* in the service of the Lord—buying a book or some *prasādam,* or giving some donation to facilitate others’ devotional service; they offer themselves in the service of the *saṅkīrtana* mission, making great endeavors to assist where they can; giving up personal considerations, they travel far distances to come early in the morning to chant *japa* in the temple with other devotees; they make tremendous sacrifices simply to serve and to please, offering themselves wholeheartedly in the service of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. We marvel at the situation and wonder, How is it possible?

And we conclude that it is because of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are actually the one doing the preaching. You are attracting everyone to Kṛṣṇa. The duty of your faithful followers is simply to bring others into contact with you. And that means we must become your pure representatives. The more purely we link people up with you by taking your instructions to heart and distributing them to others, the more effectively we help you in your mission..

You explained that “purity is the force.” What is that purity? In your preface to *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* you explained:

Some \[parents of devotees\] said that it is greatly fortunate for the Americans that I have started the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in America. But actually the original father of this movement is Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself, since it was started a very long time ago but is coming down to human society by disciplic succession. If I have any credit in this connection, it does not belong to me personally, but it is due to my eternal spiritual master, His Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya 108 Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja Prabhupāda. If personally I have any credit in this matter, it is only that I have tried to present *Bhagavad-gītā* as it is, without any adulteration.

You further explained in a lecture in Hyderabad in November 1972:

People say so much about me, that I have done some wonderful thing. But I say that I am not a magician. I’m not a magician. My only credit is that I am presenting Kṛṣṇa as He is. That’s all. I am not diluting Kṛṣṇa. That is not my business. And therefore, because it is pure, pure ghee, therefore everyone accepts. And if you place *dalda,* mixing with ghee some rascal thing, then nobody will accept. Therefore, so many went before me in the Western countries, and they presented adulterated, and there was not a single person became a *kṛṣṇa-bhakta*. Now, by thousands they are becoming. Why? Because it is presented pure thing. Pure thing will be accepted everywhere. I give this example. In a, in Delhi, there is…I have seen. One, there is confectioner’s shop. He rigidly prepares in pure ghee all the sweetmeats. So you’ll find always hundreds of customers there. And there are by the side of the that…*Dalda* manufacturer. It is not…People are still prepared to pay sufficiently if you give pure thing. So our this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is presenting *Bhagavad-gītā* as it is. We have not manufactured anything. While I started this movement in America, some friend suggested that “You are writing ‘International Society for Krishna Consciousness.’ Why not make it ‘God consciousness.’ It will be accepted by everyone.” God is Kṛṣṇa. There must, there must be clearly stated: “Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” I don’t mind if nobody accepts it, but there must be “Kṛṣṇa.”

You present Kṛṣṇa consciousness as it is. Thus the Supreme Personality of Godhead can act and persons respond and become devotees, by your purity.

Following in your footsteps, we try to represent you. Here in Cape Town, we simply wish to fully absorb ourselves in following and distributing your instructions. And thus by purely linking ourselves with you, we know that everything wonderful can be attained. We pray that you will always protect and guide us in this endeavor.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in ISKCON Cape Town, South Africa.  
(written by Rati-mañjarī-devī dāsī)

## Cebu

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet, our only shelter .

Your Divine Grace has poured your compassion upon us in a very special way here in the Hare Krishna Paradise, Cebu, Philippines.

We would like to summarize our convictions and feelings, which we have gained only through your divine mercy, by presenting to you a song composed by one of your great disciples who is sincerely dedicating his life and soul to preaching here in the Orient.

**Hero**

You are the hero of our lives,  
You are the moon in the darkness of our lives,  
You are our hero.  
You uplifted us up through the way that you act.

Your Divine Grace said, “Chant the Holy Names!  
Follow the four disciplines,  
Be patient and determined,  
Pray hard for what life really means.”

Your Divine Grace said, “Act with humility,  
Struggle with sincerity;  
Then you’ll be able to see your real self surely free.”

Your Divine Grace said,  
“Just be there, attentively hear  
And persevere;  
Then you’ll be able to feel  
The joy that is for real.”

Your Divine Grace has Kṛṣṇa,  
Who possesses all wealth.  
He has all fame;  
He carries all opulence.

Your Divine Grace has Rādhā,  
Who possesses all beauty.  
She is the full power;  
She can make us also dear to Him

You are our hero, you are our hero,  
There’s no one else who could be true,  
There’s no one else who could be true,  
There’s no one else who be true.

We thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being so tolerant and determined to take us back home to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara. Without your well-placed compassion and practical program, what hope do any of us have in this miserable condition? We are simply begging, Please make us fixed in the service of your dear disciples who sincerely follow your instructions. Please forgive us our offenses.

Thank you again and again. May our existence be a moment to moment attempt to please Your Divine Grace, and our gratitude not remain lip service. By your mercy only can we say nice prayers, and by your mercy only can we remember what our duty is in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

All glories unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
All glories to Your Divine Grace!  
All glories to your most auspicious appearance day!

The residents and congregational members who are the lowly servants of your servants at the Hare Krishna Paradise in Cebu, Philippines.

## Chamorsi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on your divine Vyāsa-pūjā day!

At Chamorsi, Maharashtra, we are developing a self-sufficient farm community for your pleasure. We have forty acres of land. This land was once a forest. We have cleared the forest and now much of the land is under cultivation.

Our GBC leaders have established the Social Development Ministry to launch *daivī-varṇāśrama*. A rural situation like we have here, with our agrarian economy, is ideal for the implementation of *daivī-varṇāśrama*.

We consider ourselves fortunate that we are in a situation where we can “depend on the land and the cows” for the pleasure of the presiding Deities of the farm, Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara.

By your mercy, last year several devotees were inspired to join us by seeing our work. The task before us now is to train them to undertake appropriate duties according to their propensities. We want them to be situated properly according to their *varṇa* and *āśrama*.

To emphasize the significance of the farm communities, you personally spent weeks on the ISKCON farms around the world. In that way, you inspired your followers to take up the farm projects seriously.

The situation is getting worse in the cities, with industrialization only increasing the people’s miseries all over the globe. We would like to see that your Chamorsi farm community can serve as an oasis in the desert of this misdirected civilization.

Please reveal your desires to us more and more. Give us the determination to become a good farm community developers, with the aim of establishing *daivī-varṇāśrama-dharma*.

Thank you for engaging us.

Your servants at ISKCON Chamorsi, India.

## Chandigarh

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances on the auspicious occasion of your divine appearance.

For your pleasure, we servants of your servants would like to humbly submit a report for the year 1997–98. Following are the details of activities at ISKCON Chandigarh.

The presiding Deities here are Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava and Gaura-Nitāi. We are trying to Them to best of our capacity via punctuality and devotion. There are regular home programs and ladies’ *sat-saṅga* to spread the message of Lord Caitanya. We regularly perform *nagara-saṅkīrtana* in different parts of city. We have fifteen devotees living in temple. Also, we have around fifty initiated devotees living outside the temple. More and more people are taking to Kṛṣṇa Consciousness here.

We participated in the *Bhagavad-gītā* distribution marathon, and we broadcast the message of the *Gītā* on local cable TV. We distributed a flyer called “Quiz on ISKCON” to inform students about your Society. The local dignitaries—the mayor, the member of parliament, and the inspector general of the police—were present at the temple here when the government of India released the postage stamp honoring you.

We have great scope for expanding the preaching here. We need to develop Nāma-hatta centers. So far we have two, one at Manimajra and another at Amritsar. During the course of last year, many people invited ISKCON to open many more centers.

We held as many festivals as possible last year. We also introduced some new festivals such as the Boat Festival and Food for Life Festival. We are actively preaching among the youth: we preached at the National Youth Festival sponsored by Punjab University, and we conducted a three-day seminar for ISKCON congregational youth in Shimla. This year we plan two Ratha-yātrās.

We conduct programs for children during holidays and, through dance dramas, teach them the philosophy of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. We had coloring competition for children. About fifteen young boys from the temple neighborhood visited the temple to study Your Divine Grace’s biography and stayed overnight for a week to attend *maṅgala-ārati.* In this way they got an idea of what it is to be a *brahmacārī* away from home. ISKCON Chandigarh actively participated in Kumbha-mela at Haridwar by organizing the camp, book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, and *harināma-saṅkīrtana.* We displayed replicas of two new ISKCON temples, one in Ahmedabad and the other in Bangalore, and received a cash donation of RS 50,000 toward the same.

ISKCON Chandigarh organized a lecture for the top officials of the Indian Police Service at the prestigious Himachal Institute of Public Administration in Shimla. We also established contact with the Indian Institute of Advanced Studies and took part in a conference on *dharma* organized by the institute. Among many scholars who attended the conference, a few recalled meeting you, and one of them, Prof. Ravi Ravindra, visited our center. Dr. Paresh Joshi, a nuclear scientist working with the Tata Institute of Fundamental Research, visited our center and spoke on the importance of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The University Business School of Punjab University invited temple devotees for a seminar on economy and business. They appreciated the value of Kṛṣṇa conscious economic development and business activities. We also attended the World Sanskrit Conference, held at Bangalore, where two ISKCON devotees presented papers.

We have a small library for our congregation. We have nice gardens and lawns that attract local people. We have a *gośālā* (15 cows and 7 calves) and use cow milk to prepare nice sweet rice, *rabri,* and other milk sweets for the pleasure of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava. We are planning to completely pay off the mortgage on the property by the end of this year. We are trying to maintain the temple property by conducting regular repairs and keeping everything neat and clean. We have a bus for book distribution, a station wagon for the *gośālā,* a car for the membership program, and an autorickshaw and three scooters for temple services. Senior *sannyāsī* disciples of Your Divine Grace guide this center, and we also have a congregational committee to assist the temple management. This year a joint management board has been formed to promote close co-operation between ISKCON Chandigarh and ISKCON Kurukshetra. A North Indian Divisional Council has been formed to promote co-operation among all the ISKCON temples in North India. We fondly remember your visit to Chandigarh in 1975. This year we have started worshiping your *mūrti* on the altar.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly let us know how you want to develop this center and bless us with sufficient intelligence and devotion so that we can please you.

Your eternal servants at ISKCON Chandigarh, India.

## Christchurch

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your divine appearance in this dark material world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything in the form of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. This most wonderful temple, the beautiful Deities of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauracandra, the association of so many sincere devotees, Śrīmatī Tulasī Devī, the previous *ācāryas,* festivals like Ratha-yātrā, the holy name, *mahā-prasādam*, the Sunday feast, your transcendental books, restaurants, *harināma, gurukula—*the list seems endless. But we remain unworthy to receive such gifts, being struggling, fumbling, fighting children of Kali, born into darkness. Somehow, however, the gifts have come and we are undeservedly blessed.

We struggle, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but it is a glorious struggle. We try to see the good in each other, though envy bites us like a maddened snake. We try to push forward with the preaching work, though the desire for personal sense gratification diverts our energy. We try to be strict, though our conditioning causes us to eat too much or sleep through the morning program. But still, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you walk among us, bearing in your hands a large plate of *mahā* mercy, and you offer Kṛṣṇa to us without discrimination of caste, creed, sex, or any other material qualification. And you give us as much as we pray for, as much as we work for, as much as we cry for.

Today, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray for the strength to continue in your ISKCON society, to somehow or other rise above the mind and senses and, as a true Prabhupāda family, preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in every town and village of New Zealand.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your fallen servants at the ISKCON temple in Christchurch, New Zealand.

## Columbus

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

You built a house in which the whole world can live, and of your Columbus *yātrā* you said, “This center should never close.” Since 1969 this center has remained open, with several changes of location and many shifts of devoted members, all having one purpose in common: to fulfill your desire that the preaching continue.

In 1969 at your Columbus center you translated Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura’s song beginning

*jiva jāgo, jiv jāgo, gauracānda bole,*  
*kota nidrā jāo māyā-piśācīra kole*

You said,

Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu is asking all living entities to “Wake up.…How long you shall go on sleeping on the lap of the witch *māyā*?…You \[have\] forgotten everything under the spell of illusory energy.”…Caitanya Mahāprabhu said that “I have brought also one medicine.…This medicine can dissipate your this forgetfulness under the spell of *māyā.*…This is this chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra. You please take it.…If one simply chants Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare, his unconscious stage, or sleeping stage under the spell of illusory *māyā,* will gradually vanish.”…The whole instruction is that all sleeping members of the human society, they should take advantage of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and willingly they should accept. Then there will be immediate effect and everyone will be out of the clutches of *māyā,* and his original consciousness, or Kṛṣṇa consciousness, will be awakened, and his life will be sublime.

As we endeavor to preach on your behalf—book distribution, *harināma-saṅkīrtana*, *lakṣmī* collection, and *prasādam* distribution—we too pray to wake up. We pray to take the medicine of Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Name and the association of devotees so that we may be instrumental in assisting others to wake up to their relationship with Kṛṣṇa. By your example and teachings you have given us the formula for preaching and the inspiration to preach.

At Ohio State University the students stood up on the arms of their chairs in response to your Hare Kṛṣṇa *kīrtana*. They applauded when you told them, “Just like we go to temple, church, with a motive. We go there: ‘O God, give us our daily bread. I have come to You for my bread.’ That is not love of God. That is love of bread.” With compassion you lovingly pointed out the fault of so-called religion. Finally, someone cared enough to speak the truth and freely give the knowledge that is not found in the mundane universities. You challenged the educational system, “Where is there a department that teaches the science of the soul?”

We still have a lot to learn. We are confident that you continue to care for us and direct us through your books and representatives. You are present in the association of your disciples, and we are happy to hear from you by hearing from them, to serve you through serving them.

We are dependent on your guidance and mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we wish to please you in our preaching endeavors.

Your aspiring servants in Columbus, Ohio.

## Costa Rica

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please let us offer our most humble, prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet, which are the only shelter for the whole of mankind.

Moved by the desire to praise your abilities and chant your glories, we place at your feet our most humble work, only aim being to fulfill your most ardent desires. Now that we understand the purpose of your auspicious appearance, we want to go to the deepest hidden part of our lives and dedicate our very lives to fulfilling that purpose.

Your work is truly substantial and scientific. Many of your sincere and fixed-up followers encourage us to emulate the dedication you showed in your lifelong preaching work. As time passes, the influence of Kali-yuga becomes stronger. The desire of sense gratification stops the conditioned human beings from understanding the purpose of their lives or following the path of self-realization, which would enable them to go back to Godhead. There are many difficult obstacles impeding the development of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, but with the knowledge and powerful techniques you give us in your books, and with your perfect example, you show us how to overcome these obstacles and practice the science of God. As time progresses, we appreciate your great gifts more and more. Each day we realize more and more that your heart is full of compassion and mercy. This makes us conscious of what it means to live within this material world, and also the way to get out of it.

You are the personification of the mercy of Śrī Nityānanda Rāma. Due to your love and compassion, we *yavanas* and *mlecchas* are coming into contact with the highest level of self-realization, *bhakti*. Of course, we are neophytes in the matter of understanding your instructions and putting them into practice. But we are encouraged to know that everything is in your books—that there is nothing to add or invent. As you said, “\[My books\] will become the lawbooks for the whole of mankind.” Indeed, your glories are being recognized by society’s leaders, and someday they will lead according to the instructions in your books.

It is impossible to not be indebted to you. Also, it is impossible to not glorify your excellent, divine qualities. Now the devotees of the Costa Rican *yātrā* have the opportunity to fulfill your desires by instructing the people about you, about your teachings, and about Kṛṣṇa. It is certainly not an easy task, and in the same way that you taught by your example, we have to teach by ours—by honestly sacrificing our self-interest and working to please Kṛṣṇa, remembering that we are not the body and forgetting the demands of it. In the place where devotees live, following the rules and regulations in pure consciousness, they eagerly hear and chant the glories of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. In that place, one who gets a chance to hear this constant flow of nectar, which is exactly like the waves of a river, will forget the necessities of life—namely hunger and thirst—and become immune to all kinds of fear, lamentation, and illusion. We humbly ask you to always give us your mercy so we may participate in your movement by understanding, practicing, and spreading your teachings around the world.

There is no one like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You have planted the seed of a civilized society by starting your movement, and the plant that has grown from that seed will one day give its fruits, as you desired. Therefore we identify ourselves as active members of this great family, whose last name is “dāsa.” We pray for your mercy so we may serve your lotus feet by spreading your precepts. Blessed Śrīla Prabhupāda, if it is your will and that of Lord Kṛṣṇa, we ask that you continue illuminating our lives in any way you desire. May your will manifest through us.

Your servants in the Costa Rica yatra.

## Czech Republic

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*śrī-caitanya-mano-’bhīṣṭaṁ sthāpitaṁ yena bhū-tale*  
*svayaṁ rūpaḥ kadā mahyaṁ dadāti sva-padāntikam*

**“When will Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī Prabhupāda, who has established within this material world the mission to fulfill the desire of Lord Caitanya, give me shelter of his lotus feet.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the perfect *rūpānuga mahā-bhāgavata paramahaṁsa ācārya*. You have an intimate contact with the lotus feet of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī Prabhupāda. Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī established a mission to fulfill the desire of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu more then four hundred years ago. You established the very same mission within the last twelve years of your earthly presence before rejoining your beloved Lord Kṛṣṇa in Goloka Vṛndāvana.

Your appearance day is the auspicious occasion for us to express our gratitude to Your Divine Grace. You are the spiritual master of the universe, our perfect guide, our true shelter. Who can understand your greatness? Lord Kṛṣṇa placed all His divine opulences in your divine hands. The limited mundane dictionary can only inadequately express your countless transcendental qualities with such words as *surrender, humility, faith, dedication, compassion, austerity, self-control, determination, tolerance, kindness, mercy, fearlessness, devotion, practicality.* You are expert in every field and perfect in every act. And Lord Kṛṣṇa empowered you to spread His glories by chanting His holy names and teaching His message.

The mission you have established for this purpose and named ISKCON goes on fulfilling the desire of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Your teachings—translated, published, and distributed in the form of BBT publications—are having an enormous impact on the world’s. The number of books distributed in all these years are tremendously influential in awakening people’s spiritual consciousness. Again and again we relish the pastimes of book distribution recounted by your disciples and grand-disciples. These pastimes are true and deeply realized pastimes of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. And you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, are both the immediate and remote cause for these pastimes to manifest. On this auspicious day, we pray that we may acquire a fraction of the humility you showed as you fulfilled your mission of fulfilling the mission of Lord Caitanya. And, as Lord Caitanya’s representative, only you could disperse the darkness of ignorance in this Age of Kali. It is said in the *śāstras* that there are many glittering stars in the sky, but the moon, though one, easily dispels the darkness that the numerous stars cannot. Such is the greatness of your personality!

Let us always remain the insignificant servants of the moonlike Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your humble servants in the Czech Republic.  
(written by Vṛndāvanī-devī dāsī)

## Dallas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the servant of Kṛṣṇa, the disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta, the spiritual master of the whole world, the saint of Vedic religion, the founder of the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, the scholar of devotional scripture, the author of transcendental books, the manager of hundreds of temples, the evangelist of Lord Caitanya’s message, the singer of divine songs, and the chanter of the holy names.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we offer respectful and humble obeisances at your lotus feet again and again and again, for not only are you our guru—training us, disciplining us, and teaching us—you are transforming the world, respiritualizing the materialistic Kali-yuga.

Therefore we at Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī Dhāma declare it boldly: His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda is indeed the Messiah for now, the Christ of our age, the long-awaited messenger of the Almighty, to be honored and glorified not only on Vyāsa-pūjā—his holy appearance day, the day following Lord Kṛṣṇa’s appearance day, Janmāṣṭamī—but every day from dawn to dawn.

We exist by your mercy alone, O divine being, divine grace, divine doorway to the kingdom of God!

Like Lord Jesus Christ before him, Prabhupāda exemplifies all that is possible, all that’s best in a human being, all that is to be emulated, the example to be copied, the life pattern to be followed, by youth and maturity. Prabhupāda, the perfect role model for future generations, whose lotus footprints are for all to follow.

In the *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is,* which Prabhupāda perfectly translated and illumined, Lord Kṛṣṇa characterizes the self-realized human being in over a tenth of the 700 verses (Bg. 2.53, 54, 56-58, 69–72; 3.13, 17, 18, 21, 25; 4.2, 10, 19-22, 24, 34; 5.7, 18-21, 26, 29; 6.8-10, 17, 19, 20-23, 29, 30, 32, 47; 7.17-19, 28-30; 8.5, 8, 14, 15; 9.13, 14, 22; 10.3; 11.55; 12.2, 7; 13.29, 32, 35; 14.22-26; 15.5, 20; 16.1-3; 17.14-16; 18.42, 43, 49, 51-56, 68, 69).

In every verse we see you, Prabhupāda. Some of these characteristics were found in saints and sages of the past, but you were with us in the present, in those magical years of ’67–’77.

For although we were born in spiritual darkness, you brought the enlightening torch of knowledge, spiritual information, the antidote to Māyā’s poison. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, brought us faith and knowledge. And you continue to inspire your followers, your readers, your disciples, grand-disciples, great-grand-disciples, and on and on, that we may follow your lotus footprints to the kingdom of God, to God’s divine devotional service, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotional service—pure, selfless, personal, nectar-filled, beneficial, truthful, and totally necessary! Revealing to us, Prabhupāda, who we really are, our spiritual identities, and giving us the means and goal in the chanting of the holy names.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, Prabhu Nityānanda, Śrī Advaita, Gadādhara, Śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda

Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare

Thank you a million times a million for this transcendental, ever-sweet refreshment. We pray for your continued blessings that we might get rid of our offenses, rectify our miserly mentalities, and better be able to hear about you and your dealings with others, your devotees, and your enthusing all living beings in the universe to revive their Kṛṣṇa consciousness, engaging everyone, to the best of their talents and tendencies, to participate in this mission.

All Glories to Prabhupāda who is, taking us back to Godhead! All Glories to Prabhupāda!

Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī Dhāma, in Dalls, Texas.  
(written by Prajāpati dāsa)

## Dresden

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

“And Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mission is to make the whole world Kṛṣṇa conscious,” my spiritual master Harikeśa Swami recently said in a lecture. Although I had heard this several times, I was stunned: One man could turn the minds of *all* people in the direction he wants?! Incredible! Many persons had tried this in the past, but they failed. And a materialist would think, “It is impossible for a single man of his age to convert even a few people to his creed.” Even a transcendentalist would have problems believing this could happen in this very fallen Age of Kali.

But it will happen. We will see it—and we can see it happening even now: In many big cities there are ISKCON temples, millions of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books are on the shelves of innumerable people, and the beginning of the construction of the Māyāpur temple, which will be a significant milestone in history, is fast approaching. All that is happening only thirty-two years after His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda came to the Western world. (Remember that an era of increasing Kṛṣṇa consciousness for the next ten thousand years is predicted!) We will see how the world will change by simply Śrīla Prabhupāda desiring it to happen.

If one looks deeper, one can see that the most important thing that had to take place before the prediction carried down for millenniums through disciplic succession (of making the whole world Kṛṣṇa conscious) could be fulfilled was that Śrīla Prabhupāda had to become the founder-*ācārya* of the ISKCON. Therefore Harikeśa Swami once used the term “Brahma-Madhva-Gauḍīya-Prabhupāda-sampradāya” to emphasize the extraordinary position of Śrīla Prabhupāda: Brahmā was the first who received the knowledge from Kṛṣṇa; Madhvācārya established the real *siddhānta*—love of Godhead—for the following generations in Bhāratavarṣa; Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu showed us the perfect way of living this knowledge; and Śrīla Prabhupāda finally delivered this matchless gift to the whole world.

Now it is incumbent on us to deliver this gift to everyone. Therefore we are hoping that Śrīla Prabhupāda will go on engaging us in his service in Dresden, our Prabhupāda-deśa. We cannot take big steps here, but if we and every center or temple around the world are doing what we are able to, the success of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mission—to make the whole world Kṛṣṇa conscious—will be certain.

Your ever-thankful servants in Dresden, Germany.

## East Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Today is your Vyāsa-pūjā, and it is the duty of the disciple to offer homage to his spiritual master on the anniversary of his appearance. I would like to present this offering not just out of duty, but out of love.

You have said that real love is unconditional—one loves another person no matter how the person behaves. Therefore, I cannot say that my love for you is unconditional. I love you because of who you are, what you’ve done, and how you’ve always behaved. I love you because of your deeds, your compassion, and your wonderful personality. I love you because of your transcendental humor, your brilliant smile, and your fatherly caring. I love you because of your quick-witted answers, your perfect movements, and your divine patience. But I love you most of all because of your unconditional love for us, your disciples and grand-disciples. You have not stopped loving and caring about us, and I am living proof of that.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

At present I am in the Ukraine for twenty days. A question asked of me was “What is your greatest realization?” I said I will tell you my latest realization. Before class, during *kīrtana,* I realized that when Śrīla Prabhupāda stepped off the *Jaladuta* in Boston harbor on September 17, 1965, he started the Kṛṣṇa consciousness ball rolling. All these devotees in Ukraine (and there are a great number of them) are enthusiastically hearing and chanting about Kṛṣṇa due to your stepping off the *Jaladuta* in Boston on September 17, 1965. All the devotees in the CIS (the former Soviet Union) are hearing and chanting about Lord Kṛṣṇa because you stepped off the *Jaladuta* in Boston on September 17, 1965. All around the world, in every temple, on many streets, people are hearing, chanting, and distributing the message of Lord Caitanya because you stepped off the *Jaladuta* in Boston on September 17, 1965. There is a temple in East Hartford, Connecticut, with a Sunday feast and devotees getting initiated, only because you stepped off the *Jaladuta* in Boston in 1965. Everything that happens in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world began by you stepping off the *Jaladuta*. My latest realization is that millions of people are becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious because you surrendered to your spiritual master and never gave up. There are others coming from India and preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness in America, Europe, and around the world. They are all doing it by your mercy only. They are following in your wake. Without you getting everything set, getting it all ready, putting devotees in place, none of them would be able it make it on their own. You were the empowered one who single-handedly spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, and you are still doing it now and will do it in the future. I pray that we may have half a sesame seed of your determination in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. If that should happen, then this world will be yours in no time.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at the East Hartford, Connecticut, temple.

## Ekaterinburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkness of ignorance, but my spiritual master opened my eyes and dissipated the darkness with the torch of knowledge. I bow down before him with deep respect.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I was born in a place where the word “God” was forgotten for many, many years. But the questions “Who am I?” and “What is life’s goal?” had always been in my heart. And when the situation was changed, I met a devotee who showed me your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*. I had already heard much about this book and was happy to buy it. From that time my life changed. I became very interested in your books, and in them I found all the answers to my questions. I accepted you as my rescuer and benefactor.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books have opened a wonderful world before me. They have shown me that our real home is Goloka Vṛndāvana, the spiritual world, and that we can make this world like the spiritual world if we all live in peace and serve Kṛṣṇa together, simultaneously trying to love and serve one another in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. In this way we can satisfy the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the supreme master of all the worlds.

Following the injunctions of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, you gave us a process of devotional service centering on the congregational chanting of the Holy Names of God. By this process all people can be happy in this world and ultimately reach the goal of human life—pure love of God and passage to the kingdom of God. All over the world you opened many beautiful temples and installed beautiful Deities, and in those temples and others opened after your departure anyone can come and learn the science of devotional service, get the blessings of the Lord, associate with devotees, chant the Holy Names, and make their lives perfect.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I have visited many temples and attended many festivals in our country and abroad, and I have seen your large, multinational, friendly family. So many people have taken and are taking shelter of your lotus feet and making their lives auspicious. You have perfectly connected the prosperity of the West with the perfect philosophy of the East, and it has produced wonderful results.

I believe that by your mercy and blessings the world will blossom and live in love with God. You have so many sincere followers who can inspire others to follow the path of *bhakti-yoga* by their presentation of your teachings and by their own example.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the ideal servant of the Lord and of everyone. May you always be loved, served, and glorified throughout all three worlds!

“Wherever there is Kṛṣṇa, the master of all mystics, and wherever there is Arjuna, the supreme archer, there will also certainly be opulence, victory, extraordinary power, and morality. That is my opinion” (*Bhagavad-gītā* 18.78).

Your aspiring servants in New Ekacakra, in Ekaterinburg, Russia.  
(written by Bhaktin Elena Goncharenko)

## Enugu

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your all-auspicious lotus feet. Thank you for sending us His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Swami, who has found shelter at your lotus feet.

On this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day, I am writing to express our profound gratitude to you for the trouble you took upon yourself to deliver millions of Jagāis and Mādhāis. It is rare to find your kind. Although we did not meet you physically, the abundant literature written about you and the many personal accounts of your pastimes make it obvious that your love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa is unfathomable. And that potent love has spread into unlimited branches and is still spreading in all directions, overwhelming the three worlds.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is most regrettable that although you have given us such a rare gift—Kṛṣṇa consciousness—we do not know its value and are not taking full advantage of it. We pray that, despite our shortcomings, by your abounding mercy we will take full advantage of the process of devotional service you have given us.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya Jagad Guru!

The servants of your servants in Enugu, Nigeria.

## Ghana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, it is appropriate to soberly reflect on the greatness of your mercy and mission on this planet.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, before your divine appearance, *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* the *dharma* for this age, was practiced only within the confines of India. But Your Divine Grace mercifully spread the holy names across the globe within such a short period of time, purifying the ether and bringing peace to a troubled world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, Lord Kṛṣṇa spoke the *Bhagavad-gītā* fifty centuries ago, but the people of the world take little cognizance of this great transcendental literature until Your Divine Grace appeared and presented the *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*. Today, the *Bhagavad-gītā* has been accepted the world over as the best of all scriptures.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, prior to your divine appearance, many great personalities who were strong proponents of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* descended to propagate this transcendental science, yet it remained unknown to the world outside India. Now by our causeless mercy people the world over have easy access to this science through your books and sincere followers.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your divine appearance, you have kindly established many places of pilgrimage on the face of the globe by erecting huge temples and initiating gorgeous Deity worship. Today, millions of souls visit these temples for spiritual upliftment. In addition, you have revealed all the important holy *dhāmas* in India for the benefit of your followers.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your divine mercy, the world has witnessed and continues to witness the colorful and soul-inspiring festivals of Ratha-yātrā, Janmāṣṭamī, Gaura Pūrṇimā, etc., which were hardly heard of before your preaching exploits. Today the world is blessed by the regular celebration of such festivals.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the unique program of profusely distributing *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* to hungry people has assumed a global status and is purifying the minds and hearts of those who come forward to accept this mercy.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your unprecedented divine mercy you have transformed low-class *mlecchas* and *yavanas* into pure Vaiṣṇavas, who have now become your instruments in fulfilling your divine mission.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have wonderfully created an international family of devotees that gives everyone an opportunity for saintly association, learned consultation, and practical participation.
