Indeed, you have built a transcendental shelter for your followers of yesterday, today, and tomorrow.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your powerful preaching, atheistic scientists, antagonistic demons, and staunch Māyāvādī philosophers have all come to accept the supremacy of Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are very much limited in our attempt to glorify your unlimited and ever-expanding glories. Please, therefore, kindly accept whatever few words of glorification we are able to offer on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance.

Thank you for mercifully coming into the material world to deliver all conditioned souls, including us, the most fallen of all.

Your grand-disciples in Ghana, West Africa.

## Gothenburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Somehow or other we have had the fortune to come in contact with you through your books or through your enthusiastic followers. Amazing it is, and very rare, like the tortoise coming up to the surface from the deep ocean and putting his head through a hole in a piece of driftwood. The chance of it happening is very small, but it did for us fortunate souls. Fortunate we were not only to be in the right place at the right moment, but also to have the blessings to somewhat understand and appreciate you and your teachings. Most souls here do not have that fortune. They more or less don’t care.

No wonder that when Śrī Kṛṣṇa asked you to come here to the material world, you were a little hesitant to go. This whole manifestation is full of those who work against you and your message. Everywhere there are souls struggling to enjoy, conquer, and control. They are stepping on each other, dragging each other down, even killing each other, only to be able to enjoy some temporary sensation of the body and mind, not seeing that everything they achieve is doomed to failure. As said in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 2.1.4:

*dehāpatya-kalatrādiṣv ātma-sainyeṣv asatsv api*  
*teṣāṁ pramatto nidhanaṁ paśyann api na paśyati*

**“Persons devoid of** ***ātma-tattva*** **do not inquire into the problems of life, being too attached to the fallible soldiers like the body, children, and wife. Although sufficiently experienced, they still do not see their inevitable destruction.”**

Explaining the source of this ignorance, you write in your purport:

“It is all due to ignoring the constitution of the living soul.…The whole materialistic advancement of human civilization is like the decoration of a dead body. Everyone is a dead body flapping only for a few days.”

Having received your causeless mercy, we can clearly see that the only meaningful activity in this prison house of the material world—the only activity that gives real satisfaction—is to take part in your and Lord Caitanya’s transcendental *saṅkīrtana* revolution. No other activity here is of any use; it’s only the “flapping of dead bodies.” There are so many ways we can waste our precious time, but they only entangle the soul deeper and deeper in pain and grief. It’s painful to play with fire, and the only solace for a burned soul is the cooling care of the moonlike Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and His pure servant.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us engage more and more in your service, and also help us inspire others to do the only sane thing: to surrender fully at your lotus feet. Let us help bring as many souls as possible home to the beautiful land of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa—the land of Vṛndāvana, where everything is centered on the beautiful Lord and His pastimes, and all live free of pride and envy.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to you!

*Your servants at the Gothenburg temple, Sweden.*

## Guadalajara

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Today, as every year, we come before you and try to express, through this humble Vyāsa-pūjā offering, the unlimited gratitude we feel for all you’ve done for us. We also pray to you for the determination to continue trying to follow your instructions, most specifically your instruction to systematically propagate the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Covered by *māyā,* we tried for countless lifetimes to adapt ourselves to this miserable material world and live here peacefully. But it was impossible, for we were all invariably defeated by the time factor. Yet still we remained attached to remain in it. But now the big difference is that we have come to rely on your constant and expert guidance, not only to remove ourselves from this material world, but to help others get out as well. By distributing your books we have the chance to help others and, without having any qualification, get both your blessings and Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s. What you have done with us and for us is truly wonderful and a work of genius, although it is difficult to fathom it in practice.

Much time has passed and our work has advanced very little. To mature in life takes time, but whatever we have accomplished, thanks to you, has been very effective because we have not matured as materialists (fortunately we failed) but as your servants, although by qualification we could not have even dreamed of it. In spite of our shortcomings, we humbly pray for your mercy, which no one can deny is causeless.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, although we are disqualified, you have given us a lifetime of service to try to become qualified *brāhmaṇas* and guide society, lest it be lost in ignorance. Such a task for persons in our category is monumental, practically impossible, but somehow or other you felt sure that with a serious and honest effort to remain in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we could do it. And although time has gone by, we have not lost sight of the spiritual horizon before us. On the contrary, it is more clearly defined, not by our efforts, but because our desire to serve you has increased Therefore, your blessings are essential.

Today we are gathered to bow down before your lotus feet and to try, in our humble way, to glorify you through this offering and become worthy of your mercy. Please give us your blessings so that we can fulfill your mission and make our lives successful.

Your servants in Nueva Nilācala Mandir, Guadalajara, Mexico.

## Guyana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this auspicious day of your appearance, we in Guyana have a confession to make. Your movement has been here for more than twenty years, but we have not achieved the degree of success we and you desire. Over the last ten years or so, we have not made many *bhaktas* or distributed many books, and *prasādam* distribution is lacking. It does not seem to be the fault of the people of this country. They are friendly, pious, and very favorable, a third of the population being of Hindu stock. It seems that the fault lies with us. Just as the gross materialists cannot conceive that because of their lack of devotion they cannot understand God, so too it is because of our puffed-up nature and lack of devotion and dedication to your mission that we are unsuccessful in expanding your movement by leaps and bounds, given so many favorable assets. We tend to always try to place the blame on someone else.

Added to so many favorable situations, today we can see other manifested fruits of your blessings. Since the early 80’s, Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Caitanyacandra have presided at Uitvlugt, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulacandra have presided at New Pāṇihāṭī, in Berbice. In Essequibo there are Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva, at New Navadvīpa Dhāma.

In spite of so many favorable assets, our association here has been very thin. This you recognized, and so over the last few years you have mercifully sent senior devotees to guide and inspire us. Vīrabāhu and Śeṣa Prabhus have come here regularly. Your only disciple from Guyana, Bhūtādi Prabhu, has decided to take up residence here again. Many *sannyāsīs* are visiting on a regular basis.

Through the inspiration of your dear sons, we are coming to realize that it is not enough simply to remain fallen, unqualified, stumbling, and babbling. Rather, the goal of our existence is to serve and fulfill your mission to make ISKCON grow. Then at the time of death we can look back and say, “Yes, we did something for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda; we didn’t waste our valuable human lives selfishly.”

Despite our material conditioning and neophyte mood, your small band of faithful ISKCON followers are determined to maintain and improve the Guyana *yātrā*. This year two massive temple projects are in being constructed. The few devotees in the Berbice area are dedicating their blood, sweat, and tears to completing, by this year’s Vyāsa-pūjā, a 105 x 75 foot Vedic-style temple, which will be the new home for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulacandra in New Kulinagram. And on the west coast of Demerara, devotees are starting a new temple to house Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Caitanyacandra.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us so we can maintain and expand what you have given us. Please bless us to remain strong in our daily *sādhana-bhakti* and not cheaply consider ourselves advanced devotees. Please bless us to be free from fault-finding and from “groupism.” Bless us to stay in the association of devotees. Please wake us up, smash us, crash us, do whatever it takes to make us sincere about Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

If sincerity is the qualification for having you answer our prayers, then we are in deep trouble, since we have very little. Still, we are hoping that because we speak your holy name we will catch your attention. Thank you for your affection, commitment, connection, sincerity, honesty, and compassion. Thank you for giving us Kṛṣṇa consciousness, complete with Deity worship, sixteen rounds, and *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*. Thank you for allowing our temples to manifest. Thank you for our very lives.

Your humble servants in Guyana.

## Hardwar

Our most dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, O great divine personality, O savior of the whole world, Śrīla Prabhupāda! We pray at your lotus feet to give us the strength to glorify you, the most magnanimous divine personality, because we have realized that it is the most difficult task in the three worlds.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us ISKCON and your extremely dedicated devotee His Holiness Navayogendra Swami—these are our only hope for survival in this most dangerous age of quarrel (Kali-yuga).

We are experiencing the pleasure of Goloka Vṛndāvana, as mentioned in the scriptures you’ve given. We are also experiencing Śrī Gaura-līlā every day and night.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful unto us most fallen souls and keep us fixed in your transcendental mission of spreading the holy name of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and Sītā-Rāma to each and every soul on this planet till we leave this world and finally go back home, back to Godhead, where you are waiting for all of us.

Finally, please accept a few of our tears and grant us pure love for you, which is nondifferent from the love of Śrī Gaurāṅga and Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Please give us shelter in the shade of your lotus feet.

The eternal servants of your servants, the devotees at ISKCON Hardwar.

## Hong Kong

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It’s now the most difficult time for us to face. But your words are always giving us solace. You said that preaching is meant to make an unfavorable situation favorable. So you have given us a heavy task: to put all our energy, time, and effort into serving your mission of fulfilling Lord Caitanya’s desire to see Kṛṣṇa consciousness spread to every town and village.

We are most fallen, yet you have given us the highest good fortune—Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And we know that you will continue to provide this fortune, forever and ever. This fortune is now being delivered by your great-minded followers, who represent your wish to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all around the world. How much we appreciate this gift of yours, and how much we will be able to help fulfill your mission, depend on how much we take advantage of the association of your representatives. Undoubtedly, you are living in the hearts of your followers whose desires are one with yours. We cannot try to accept the instructions in your books but neglect the instructions of your great followers; then we will understand neither. Your teachings are confirmed by the eternal principle of guru, *sādhu,* and *śāstra*. We may be living in the most unfortunate place at the most unfortunate time amid the most unsteady political and economic situation, but we feel most fortunate because we have your books, your tapes, and the association of your glorious followers.

Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jay! Gaura-bhakta-vṛnda ki jay!

Your servants in Hong Kong.

## Israel

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Taking a straw between our teeth, we are happy to glorify Your Divine Grace on the auspicious celebration of your Vyāsa-pūjā. We may have only a very limited realization of your glories, but we are nevertheless happy to even attempt to glorify you and to reflect upon our relationships with you. We like to remember that you planned to visit Israel just before you went to Moscow; you even sent your disciples here to prepare for your visit. But somehow or other Israel never got your *vapu*.

However, your *vāṇī* we got, and we have tried to distribute the mercy for your pleasure. For your pleasure, hundreds of thousands of books and profuse *prasādam* have been distributed, many hours of *harināma* performed, and different types of preaching programs conducted. Many devotees have joined your movement here, and the *mahā-mantra* has become famous all over Israel, among Jews and Arabs, Druze and Africans.

We have to be honest and admit that there have been some failures along with the successes. We have failed in many respects to please Your Divine Grace. We have proved to be bound by our tendency toward politics and have quarreled among ourselves. We have wasted a lot of energy on trivial matters and failed to cooperate in rendering you service.

Still, we are firmly convinced of the basic principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, as you have taught: Preaching is the essence, books are the basis, purity is the force, and utility is the principle. Your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* and *Nectar of Devotion* are our main source of scriptural inspiration. ISKCON is our home, the GBC our authority. You built a house for the whole world to reside. Let us continue to construct one room for Israel. You gave us your books: Let us distribute them and teach them to the people of Israel. You taught us how to worship Gaura-Nitāi and offer them *bhoga*. Let us worship Them and distribute Their *prasādam* in mass quantities.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, low and unqualified though we may be, please bestow your mercy upon us. We want to be Kṛṣṇa conscious, and we want to be your eternal servants. Please accept us as such.

Your servants in Israel.  
(written by Īśvara Kṛṣṇa dāsa)

## Jos

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our insignificant but humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

All glories to your divine lotus feet! All glories to the precious *ācāryas,* and all glories to Śrī Guru and Śrī Gaurāṅga!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your all-auspicious appearance day is again affording us the opportunity to glorify Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who manifests Himself on earth in different ways and whose manifestation as the Supersoul and subsequently as the spiritual master is the highest manifestation of His mercy. For by so doing He descends to the level of us sinful living entities just to purify us and reclaim us back to His eternal abode, and you are the embodiment of this manifestation.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, since there cannot be enough words with which to glorify you for your endless mercy on us, we only beg that you continue to grant us shelter at your lotus feet.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Vyāsa-pūjā!

Your unworthy grandsons in Jos, Nigeria.

## Krakow

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

This is an extremely auspicious and pleasing occasion for everyone because you appeared on this day. Without the presence of such a *mahātmā* as yourself, the entire world would be bereft of auspiciousness. It is said in all authentic revealed scriptures that actual auspiciousness begins for living entities when they come in touch with pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Your magnanimous contribution of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the world will serve as the superlative example for all future generations.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the most dear disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja because you embraced his ideas of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness on a worldwide basis. And you are the only one with sufficient broadness of heart and mind to endeavor and succeed in planting the Kṛṣṇa consciousness culture all over the world, particularly in the Western world. While you enacted your pastimes here, you were absorbed in spreading *sanātana-dharma* even though external circumstances were sometimes extremely unfavorable. In this way you displayed your firm faith in Kṛṣṇa and Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja.

Even now it is due to your mercy and vision that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is spreading more and more n Poland, despite our propensity for sense gratification. Too often we foolishly try to embrace *māyā,* though every time she kicks us strongly. We beg you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us enough strength and intelligence to become some insignificant part of your dream to spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu in Poland.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bestow your mercy upon us so that we may be able to serve you. We have no shelter other than your lotus feet.

Your humble servants in Krakow, Poland.

## Lagos

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your splendidly radiant lotus feet. All glories to your Divine Grace!

Throughout the world, at this most auspicious appearance-day celebration of Your Divine Grace, stalwart devotees are pouring forth eloquent eulogies glorifying the wonderful *līlāmṛta* of Your Divine Grace. But we are bereft of the transcendental poetry to properly glorify the unfathomable activities of Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as your grandchildren, we earnestly appeal to you to forgive us for our nonsense. Your son, His Holiness Bhakti Tīrtha Swami, loves us so much that he is sacrificing his life for us, but you, our spiritual grandfather, love us even more. We pray, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that our minds may always remember your superexcellent qualities, our bodies always render various services to you and your mission, and our words always describe your glories. Please cast your merciful glance upon us.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your grandchildren at ISKCON Lagos, Nigeria.  
(written by Parīkṣit dāsa and Vāsudeva dāsa)

## Leicester

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

On this most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day we would like to express our gratitude for your continuing presence, guidance, protection, inspiration, chastisement, and help. Much has been accomplished this year due to your causeless mercy and the mercy and help of your disciples. When focusing on you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we find that everything seems to become so easy and clear and all contradictions are immediately resolved. You are the root cause of everything in our spiritual lives and of all we have been able and allowed to do for your pleasure.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have not only planted the *bhakti-latā-bīja* in our hearts, but you have also wisely designed a structure and a network enabling us to nourish and protect this devotional creeper long after your physical departure. In fact, you continue to carry out your mission for your spiritual master and the entire *paramparā* through your ISKCON movement, through the instructions left in your books, and through the mind, intelligence, and bodies of your dedicated and faithful followers. We are reminded of the exchange between Lord Caitanya and Sanātana Gosvāmī:

\[Lord Caitanya said:\] “Your body is My principal instrument for executing many necessary functions. By your body I shall carry out many tasks.”

\[Sanātana Gosvāmī replied:\] “A wooden doll chants and dances according to the direction of a magician but does not know how he is dancing and singing. My dear Lord, as You cause one to dance, he dances accordingly, but how he dances and who is causing him to dance he does not know.” \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, *Antya* 4.78, 85–86\]

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most merciful and intimate servitor of the independent Supreme Lord, and we are exactly like wooden dolls dancing as you make us do so.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for coming down from the spiritual world to teach us how to get back home.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for coming to the West to establish this ISKCON movement.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the association of your disciples and grand-disciples.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the *mahā-mantra*.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for creating a house in which the whole world can live.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for teaching us how to do *kīrtana* and *bhajana*.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for inaugurating Ratha-yātrā.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us a temple in Leicester, so that we can associate with devotees and teach others as you taught us.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us Śrī Pañca-tattva, so that we can engage in their divine service and dance in ecstasy.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for letting us worship your *mūrti* in your physical absence, so that we can always remember you.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us your transcendental books.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for showing us a glimpse of your humility by your personal example.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the opportunity to hear and learn from you or through your disciples.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the opportunity to pass on what we have learned.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for setting the standard for the next ten thousand years.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for letting us play a part in your ever-growing ISKCON family.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for making accessible the holy *dhāmas* of Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for installing so many Deities all over the world.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your Bhaktivedanta purports, which are so clear, powerful, and strong that they can cut through the ignorance of any rascal.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us so many festival days and for showing us how to celebrate them.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us Nāma-haṭṭa and congregational preaching and development.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the association of so many wonderful devotees all around the globe via e-mail and COM conferences.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us *prasādam* and for teaching us how to prepare and offer it.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us *gurukula* education for our children.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for letting us be a part of your most glorious movement.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for this divine philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, which cannot be defeated by anyone.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for touching the hearts of those who have met you personally and of those who have met those who have met you personally.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for guiding us through your *vāṇī*.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for setting up the GBC body to guide your movement through the dangerous waters of this material ocean.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the chanting of the Holy Name.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us Kṛṣṇa.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for establishing the Sunday love feast, which engages all in devotional service, either by preparing, serving, or eating it.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for rectifying deviations in the development of your ISKCON movement.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for helping us remember our real identity as eternal servitors of Kṛṣṇa.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for showing us by your personal example what unalloyed devotional service really is.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for instructing us to do the needful according to time, place, and circumstances.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for letting us never forget you.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your wonderful smile and your great humor.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your uncompromising lionlike preaching.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for showing us by your personal example how to leave this world.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for instructing us to co-operate as a sign of real love for you.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for teaching us to use everything in the service of the Lord.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us *varṇāśrama-dharma*.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for teaching us austerity and renunciation.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for teaching us the real values of life.

We thank you so much Śrīla Prabhupāda for ensuring our spiritual progress by creating a transcendental network of *dīkṣā-* and *śikṣā-gurus*.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for inspiring us to take up more and more responsibilities on your behalf.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for showing us how to save others.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the list of your glories, your teachings, your inspiration, your orders, and your strategies to coax us back into the divine service of the Lord is limited only by our own inability of expression and perhaps by the space in this Vyāsa-pūjā book. We could go on endlessly describing all the numerous and most wonderful transcendental gifts and gems you personally gave us or are giving us via the transparent medium of your faithful followers. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most magnanimous and confidential servitor of the most merciful Supreme Lord. Exactly as a magician makes wooden dolls dance, please make us dance as you desire.

Your devotees in Leicester, UK.  
(written by Gaurāṅga Sundara dāsa)

## Liverpool

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Due to your great and causeless mercy, you have given us the greatest benediction ever. You have bestowed upon us the rare and golden opportunity to return to the lotus feet of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. No other gift can compare to this gift you are giving us.

In this Age of Quarrel there is so much hypocrisy and cheating. Without your help, we wouldn’t stand a chance. Your Divine Grace is saving us from the frauds and cheats by giving us clarity, purity and truth. You gave us the following instructions:

The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is meant for achieving perfection in life. It is not a bluff or exploitation, but unfortunately people are accustomed to being bluffed. There is a verse by an Indian poet: “If one speaks nice things, people will quarrel with him: ‘Oh, what nonsense you are speaking.’ But if he bluffs them and cheats them, they will be very glad.” So if a bluffer says, “Just do this, give me my fee, and within six months you will become God,” then they will agree: “Yes, take this fee, and I shall become God within six months.” No. These cheating processes will not solve our problem. If one actually wants to solve the problems of life in this age, then he has to take to this process of *kīrtana*. That is the recommended process.

harer nāma harer nāma harer nāmaiva kevalam  
kalau nāsty eva nāsty eva nāsty eva gatir anyathā

In this age, Kali-yuga, one cannot execute any process of self-realization or perfection other than *kīrtana*. *Kīrtana* is essential in this age. \[*The Science of Self-Realization,* p. 141\]

You established a transcendental world-wide *kīrtana* party, which is spreading the Holy Name throughout every town and village. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing us to be a part of your wonderful movement.

Your servants in Liverpool, UK.  
(written by Cakra-nādi-devī dāsī)

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We, the most fortunate servants of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, come before you on this Vyāsa-pūjā day to express our gratitude for one of your special gifts, the association of your devotees.

The association of your devotees is the best and safest place to be. There we can inspire and enliven one another. There we can share together the sweet taste of the holy name.

Only with the help of your devotees can we fulfill your desires and therefore please Kṛṣṇa.

Only by learning how to deal with one another nicely, through service and humility and the example of your more advanced devotees, will our hearts become free from envy, anger, and selfishness.

Only by the association of your devotees will we be able to escape from this miserable material world and attain the eternal bliss of the spiritual world.

Only from your devotees can we receive real love and affection.

Thank you for this precious gift, the association of your devotees. Please kindly bless us that we will be able to remain together, serving together and cooperating together, for your pleasure, for the duration of our time in these bodies.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants, the servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, in London (Soho), UK.

## London (south)

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble *daṇḍavats* at the dust of you divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this the most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

We are constantly glorifying your transcendental achievements, most notably that of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world. We are constantly wondering where we would be now if it were not for Your Divine Grace had not left wonderful Vṛndāvana and worked so hard to convince the fallen, conditioned souls to stop their self-destructive, headlong rush to hell. One thing is for sure: If it were not for your sublime Bhaktivedanta purports, we would all be engaged nicely in Māyā’s kingdom, trying unsuccessfully to serve our insatiable senses.

You ignored all personal considerations such as health and comfort and decided to travel to far-off places for the benefit of the whole world. Wherever you went, that place became Goloka Vṛndāvana due to the strong power of you association. In your purports to the *Bhagavad-gītā* (8.4) you state, “Some say that the devotee should remain in holy places like Vṛndāvana or some holy town where the Lord lived, but a pure devotee can live anywhere and create the atmosphere of Vṛndāvana by his devotional service.”

Similarly, King Yudhiṣṭhira said to Viduraji in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.13.10):

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni svāntaḥ-sthena gadābhṛtā*

**“My lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage.”**

We are very thankful to Your Divine Grace and your disciples for your merciful glances, which have enabled us here at this center to continue in our quest to further your transcendental mission. We have seen many, many devotees come and go. But somehow or other the Lord continues to maintain the service afforded to Him by the devotees. We pray that by your kind blessing we can persevere with whatever little devotional service we are trying to perform and in that way please you. If the guru is pleased, then Kṛṣṇa will automatically be pleased.

*śrī-vigrahārādhana-nitya-nānā-*  
*śṛṅgāra-tan-mandira-mārjanādau*  
*yuktasya bhaktāṁś ca niyuñjato ’pi*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

*Śrī-vigraha* is the *arcā-mūrti,* the form of the Lord suitable for worship, and the disciple should worship the Deity regularly by *śṛṅgāra,* proper decoration and dressing, as also by *mandira-mārjana,* cleansing the temple. The spiritual master kindly teaches the neophyte devotee all these practices personally to help him gradually realize the transcendental name, form, qualities, and pastimes of the Lord.

Thank you for teaching us Deity worship, Śrīla Prabhupāda—and so much more!

Your insignificant grand-disciples in South London, UK.

## Longdenville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances on this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you established ISKCON to fulfill the instructions of your Guru Mahārāja, and you carefully nurtured and developed it to maturity. Step by step you taught us the process of how to attain perfection. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your mercy.

Throughout Vedic history, great souls like Prahlāda Mahārāja have manifested their compassion by trying to bring the fools and rascals of this material world back to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your ISKCON is a manifestation of your unlimited compassion for us.

Wondrous are the achievements of today’s so-called advanced materialistic society, with its space-age science and technology. Still, without Kṛṣṇa consciousness the general masses of people—from the outrageous, ignorant atheist to the anxious environmentalist, from the impersonalist to the philanthropist to and New Age religionist—are all unhappy, despite their elaborate plans for success. But you are so kind, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that as the expert captain of the ship ISKCON, you structured it in such a way that anyone who accepts your guidance now has the chance to be pulled out of their ignorance, bypass their *anarthas,* and return to the spiritual world. But it is up to us.

In the *Bhagavad-gītā* (7.3) the Supreme Personality of Godhead says,

*manuṣyāṇāṁ sahasreṣu kaścid yatati siddhaye*  
*yatatām api siddhānāṁ kaścin māṁ vetti tattvataḥ*

**“Out of many thousands among men, one may endeavor for perfection, and of those who have achieved perfection, hardly one knows Me in truth.**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are aware that you are a *nitya-siddha,* an eternally perfect devotee of the Lord, but how much are we prepared to be actually *kaścit yatati siddhaye,* those who endeavor for perfection and ply along? Frequently we jump ship, “as if by force,” become victims of *maya* enamored by the *ahaṁ mameti* principle. But as you said (paraphrasing), “We should not be surprised by how many leave our Society but by how many stay.” It is only by your mercy that we can stay, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

So we pray to always remain onboard the good ship ISKCON and engaged in discharging the innumerable duties you left for us, such as Deity worship, book distribution, *harināma-saṅkīrtana*, Nama-haṭṭa programs, Food for Life, festivals, and temple projects. In this way we can perhaps please you and appreciate just a part of an iota of your compassion and *līlā,* which are resounding throughout the three worlds.

Happy Vyāsa-pūjā, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your aspiring servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir in Longdenville, Chaguanas, Trinidad.  
(written by Viśvarūpa dāsa)

## Malmo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

We would like to express our gratitude for the trouble you have personally undertaken in order to spread the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the West. If not for you, none of us would have ever heard about Kṛṣṇa—at least none of us would be able to comprehend the truth about Him.

As a direct representative of the Lord, specifically His mercy, you have come to liberate us from the bondage of birth and death, displaying unlimited magnanimity upon us fallen souls.

Nondevotees have a propensity to imitate successful materialists to achieve their own materialistic goals. As aspiring devotees, it is most certainly impossible for us to imitate you, the most successful spiritualist, but it is certainly possible and even vital to have you as an example, a guide, by which we can measure our daily lives.

Thank you again so much for your concern about us (and all other living entities, for that matter).

We, in our humble ways, will always try to keep your instructions close to our hearts and, taking you as the most glorious example, spread the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your servants in Malmo, Sweden.

## Manchester

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. Thank you for allowing us to remain in your service for another year.

The more we try to preach on your behalf, the more we realize what a stupendous feat you achieved. There are no material arrangements or skills, nor any amount of wealth and power, that can push on the preaching of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It can only be done by a person with a pure heart who is empowered by the Lord: *kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana*. Only those who are empowered by the Lord can actually preach. Otherwise our efforts would amount to little more than developing some mundane institution with nice sentiments but no real advancement in spiritual life.

The spreading of actual Kṛṣṇa consciousness means the changing of people’s hearts—making them give up their materialistic conceptions and take sincerely to the practices of spiritual life. And that takes genuine purity of purpose. Due to our own lack of purity, we are seeing how hard it is to make even a few people become serious in spiritual life. But you, against apparently insurmountable odds, were able to change many thousands from gross materialists into devotees of the Lord. You called that a miracle, and as time goes by we are realizing just how true that is. We are struggling to change even our own hearts, what to speak of others’.

Factually we are fully dependent on your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda. *Kṛṣṇa-śakti* will be bestowed upon that person who is able to please his guru: *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*. We are therefore praying that you glance mercifully on us. Although we lack any good qualities, by some inconceivable good fortune we have become attracted to serving you and assisting your mission. Probably even in that we are impelled by some base motives, but we have at least a desire to become your pure servants. I believe you once said that even if one does not have any pure desire, one should still should pray for a desire to have that desire. Sometimes, due to stubborn and overpowering attachments, we find it difficult to pray even like that, but today is a perfect opportunity. Please see our tiny efforts to please you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and bestow your blessings upon us. Otherwise we will be entirely and hopelessly lost.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are obviously Kṛṣṇa’s very dear servant. Although the world has been slow to recognize what you have achieved, that recognition will surely come with time. Gradually, influenced by the potency of the *saṅkīrtana* movement, society will see that its materialistic values are simply madness. There are already signs of that. It is our duty now to ensure that the world recognizes your vital contribution toward this spiritual revolution: *ebe yaśa ghuṣuk tribhuvana*.

Certainly we must not, through greed and attachment, do the very opposite. We have a grave responsibility to become your pure representatives and show by our example the wonderful benefits of your teachings. Whatever anyone else may be teaching or presenting, without doubt your instructions have the potency to change hearts and save lives. We owe it to you, and to the world, to properly convey those instructions to as many people as we can. Thus we will please your Lord, Kṛṣṇa, and crown our own lives with all success.

We should not rest until this day, the day of your appearance, has become a national holiday throughout the world—Prabhupāda Day. Let it be a day when people chant the holy name and dance in the street with joyful abandon, when the governments in every nation arrange for a great feast for all the people. Well, a bit far-fetched, perhaps, but that would surely be a fitting tribute to you, and we cannot think of any other that will suffice. At least striving for such a vision will keep us busily engaged in Kṛṣṇa’s service.

Once again, then, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we thank you for your kindness. Here in Manchester it has been a difficult year, but we feel the preaching is moving forward in a positive way. Although we cannot report any big advances in our numbers—numbers of books, *harināmas,* new devotees, programs, etc.—we can at least say that we are gaining deeper realizations of what it means to be your representative, and we are trying to come up to the mark. That has got to be the first step. Once we reach that soaring and glorious mark, we will surely see great things happening.

But in the meantime we can only fall helplessly at your feet and beseech your causeless mercy. Please do not cast us aside, seeing us as the useless fools we undoubtedly are. Allow us the chance to keep trying, through so many failures, to follow your clear instructions. We thus remain,

The servants of your servants in Manchester, UK.  
(written by Kṛṣṇa Dharma dāsa)

## Manila

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and sincere *daṇḍavat* obeisances at the dust of your most cooling lotus feet.

The whole world has yet to appreciate the most sublime gift you gave us, Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Although you made yourself available—personally for a span of ten years or so and afterward through the transcendental books, temples, devotees, and movement you left us—the vast majority of people in this world still do not appreciate your gifts. Nonetheless, your position as the empowered *ācārya* for this age has never been diminished. Rather, you have proven that we are nothing without you. The invaluable guidance you have given us through your writings, lectures, and pastimes is more than enough to make us fixed up in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Doubts, envy, greed, and lusty desires are purged from our hearts by the pure and uncompromising instructions you laid down. In addition, you expertly established ISKCON temples, farm communities, *gurukulas,* restaurants—everything we would ever need for our progress in spiritual life. We feel that even if we were to combine all the accomplishments of great persons throughout history, they would not come close to matching your inconceivable achievements.

Here in the Philippines, which is known as the only Christian country in Asia, there are many opportunities to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. People in general are religious by nature and soft-hearted, making it a fertile ground in which to plant the seed of devotional service. But we also see, perhaps because of too much soft-heartedness or even weakness of heart, that in general it is difficult for Filipinos to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness continuously for a long time. And so the number of full-time devotees here has declined drastically and now stands at the bare minimum to maintain a medium-sized temple.

We therefore fervently pray for even a drop of your mercy, so that we may be steadfast, determined, tolerant, and patient in our execution of devotional service. You possess all these qualities, and many more, to the fullest extent. Therefore kindly empower us to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness so that by our humble attempt your glories will spread to all the people of the Philippines, and to every corner of the whole world as well.

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we meditate on rededicating our lives to your service, along with service to ISKCON, to the GBC, to all Vaiṣṇavas, to the *paramparā* succession, and to the spiritual masters.

May your glories be broadcast and boldly declared to all the three worlds. Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

Your aspiring grand-disciples in Manila, the Philippines.  
(written by Śrīdāmā dāsa)

## Manipur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearmost Parama Guru Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day of your divine appearance in this world, please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, to us you are the incarnation of Śrī Nārada Muni, in view of the role you played in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness worldwide. People East and West were sleeping, entangled in the material conception of life, but you awakened their dormant Kṛṣṇa consciousness and converted them into saints. Though you have physically disappeared from this material world, your contributions to this world in the field of spiritual knowledge—in the forms of books, recorded speeches, and songs—still remain. They are a guiding torch dispelling the darkness of ignorance for the very unfortunate souls like us, who are bound by worldly illusion.

We are trying to do what you wanted us to in pushing on the *saṅkīrtana* movement in this part of the world. You strongly desire that Manipur would become an ideal Vaiṣṇava state. To fulfill your desire, ISKCON Manipur has launched a very ambitious and unique project—a University of Bhāgavata Culture—under the able leadership of your beloved disciple Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami. The project consists of the following units: the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Manimandir, the Bhagyachandra Rasamandala Hall, the Vedavyasa Library, the Chaitanya College of Sankirtana, the College of Arts and Music, the Basic Training College of Brahmacharis and Vaishnava Manners, the Chitrangasa Women’s College of Vocational Training, the Babhruvahana School of Martial Arts and Vaishnava Administration, and the Bhaktivedanta Institute of Science and Culture.

We pray to you for enough strength and wisdom to carry out your divine instructions.

Your fallen servants,  
The devotees of ISKCON Manipur.

## Markham

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this day of your auspicious appearance,  
You created for us a most rare chance

To come out of the darkness  
And into the path of spiritualness.

At an early age, the astrologers uttered a prophecy:  
At the age of seventy you would put us into ecstasy.

So you set sail to a foreign land,  
Set to implement your Guru’s divine command.

From the holy *dhām*, you were so far apart,  
But you kept Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in the core of your heart.

Lord Kṛṣṇa was sending His beloved son  
To fulfill Lord Caitanya’s mission.

We wish we could tell everyone all your glories  
And recite your unlimited stories.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, even if our praises for you would touch the sky,  
Still this would not be enough to glorify.

Despite this, we will make a humble attempt  
To glorify you who have spread the holy name to every continent.

It is hard to believe how much you did fight  
To bring Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the neophyte.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so pure—  
With us undeserving souls you had to endure.

Like a merciful shower of rain,  
You freely spread the holy name.

You took us out of Māyā’s illusion,  
Alleviating us from all of our confusion.

Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, with your purity,  
Could spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness so successfully.

Nowhere on this earth could we find  
Someone like you who gave freedom to mankind.

You were the only one to set us free  
From the strong grips of Māyā in this Age of Kali.

From Zurich to Moscow to Amsterdam,  
You opened temples all across the land.

You spread the Kṛṣṇa Consciousness movement with such care,  
Creating devotees and spiritual awareness everywhere.

You put Hare Kṛṣṇa in the news,  
Showing everyone that there’s more to life than the *karmīs’* views.

You kept showing us what to do and kept on teaching,  
Knowing one day we would continue the preaching.

We always hanker to hear transcendental nectar  
As explained by you, O Spiritual Master.

Only through your purity and knowledge As It Is  
Has your movement been able to bring people to transcendental bliss

You planted the seed to allow for Kṛṣṇa consciousness to flourish.  
Across every continent, devotees continue to surface.

Of course these are only a few of the glories that you deserve,  
Now we must set our sights on how we are to continue to serve.

Under your love and instruction,  
We are finding ways to struggle and continue on.

There are many hurdles that are being thrown into the path of your movement,  
By your mercy, you are ensuring that none will be allowed to cement.

We have learned to continue to repeat  
The pure instructions you gave, which are so sweet.

We will continue to preach and do book distribution,  
Hoping to pull as many people out of this material condition.

We are simply applying what you so patiently have shown,  
Hoping to show everyone the path to their real spiritual home.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray to you each and every day.  
For what you have done, we could never repay.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please keep showering us with your mercy.  
Never let us leave your lotus feet or forget your history.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Jaya Gurudeva!

Your humble servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara Mandir in Markham, Canada.  
(written by Mīnākṣi-devī dāsī)

## Māyāpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances at your divine lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your divine appearance.

You have so kindly appeared in this world to deliver the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu for the benefit of all conditioned souls. It is by His message only that all living entities can come to achieve the supreme perfection of life by learning how to love Kṛṣṇa and His representative, Your Divine Grace.

By your causeless mercy, you have given us knowledge of Kṛṣṇa, whereby we can perfect our lives and return back home, back to Godhead. Not only have you given us knowledge of Kṛṣṇa; you have given us everything necessary for achieving perfection in spiritual life. You have given us Kṛṣṇa’s holy name, Kṛṣṇa’s *prasādam,* Kṛṣṇa’s devotees, Kṛṣṇa’s Deity form to worship, Kṛṣṇa’s dramas, and Kṛṣṇa’s words in the form of scriptures. Everything is being offered to us by Your Divine Grace.

Just as a king has emissaries that go to different countries to act on his behalf, Kṛṣṇa has made you His representative to accept all services on His behalf.

How do we, your disciples and grand-disciples, properly worship you on this most holy and auspicious day? Although you have concluded your pastimes on this earth, still you are present for those who want to associate with you.

You have assured us that you reside eternally in your instructions, and that those who follow those instructions reside eternally with you. This is your great promise to us all. Let this promise become a fire within our hearts, purifying our existence and burning like a beacon to guide us, day by day, across this material ocean, just as a lighthouse guides ships to shore. Without such a beacon, surely we would be wrecked upon the rocky shores of Kṛṣṇa’s external energy.

We understand that the proper way to worship you is to follow your instructions to the best of our abilities, regardless of our service. Whether we are sweepers of your streets or managers of your departments, distributors of your books or protectors of your cows, worshiping Your Divine Grace means to act with complete love for you, giving our hearts to you completely. Loving you means cooperating fully with each and every devotee, serving you together in all our diverse services. This will surely please you and glorify you.

You have given us residents of Śrī Dhāma Māyāpur so much special mercy by revealing this holy *dhāma* and ordering us to develop it. You ordered us to build a magnificent temple here, the Temple of the Vedic Planetarium, predicted by Lord Nityānanda Himself. You ordered us to dig the foundations before we even had any plans. After we had done so, you personally laid the cornerstone for the foundation. You wanted to show the world how important this project is to you.

We know this project is not just the piling of bricks upon bricks. It is a task of building character within ourselves, of cleansing our hearts by developing loving relationships with all devotees, great and small. Only after such a transcendentally wonderful atmosphere exists will Kṛṣṇa reveal His *adbhuta mandir*. Please help us develop such an atmosphere here in Māyāpur.

Actually, we cannot build a temple for Kṛṣṇa; the temple is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa, and Kṛṣṇa comes as He pleases. We can only cleanse our hearts by following your instructions. By doing so, Kṛṣṇa will be pleased to reveal Himself in the form of His temple.

You said Bombay is your office, Vṛndāvana your home, and Māyāpur your place of worship. We are all greatly honored to be allowed to live and serve your mission here.

You have established Śrī Dhāma Māyāpur as the international headquarters for your International Society for Krishna Consciousness, and it is our responsibility to make it exemplary in all ways.

Please give us the understanding by which we can develop this most sacred eternal residence of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and His associates, so that their fame may spread to every town and village throughout the entire world. If it pleases you to give us such service and responsibility, then we are prepared to accept them upon our bowed heads and carry forward your mission, wherever it may lead us.

We must follow principles you have established, maintain what you have developed, and assist Your Divine Grace and your disciples in developing Śrī Dhāma Māyāpur. In this way, the entire world may come to know the glories of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and all living entities can come to the stage of perfection.

We humbly fall at your divine lotus feet and sincerely beg and pray that you will empower us to know your desires, understand your mood, accept your instructions, and implement your divine will. Please help us learn how to serve everyone who comes to take *darśana* of the Dhāma and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava, Śrī Pañca-tattva, Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva, and Their associates. Please continue to shower your blessings upon all of us by giving us more and more service to your divine lotus feet. We know and understand that continued service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, through you, is the proof of your satisfaction, the gift of Your Divine Grace upon us. We pray to you again and again that you may continue to be pleased upon us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are completely satisfied simply to know that you have accepted our service. We know that whatever success we may receive is due only to your divine blessings, and to those of your empowered disciples, coming from you.

We humbly beg that you always keep us under the shade of your divine lotus feet. Please continue to give us an opportunity to render you service, so that we may become more and more enthusiastic to carry forward your mission, to develop here the spiritual world you have given us.

Please help us learn the proper meaning of Vaiṣṇava-seva. Show us how to serve and protect each and every one of your disciples and grand-disciples, so they may be happy and content to serve you in all circumstances. Help us make Māyāpur such a wonderful community of Vaiṣṇavas that one will never think of leaving your divine service, or the association of your devotees. Please inculcate within each and every one of us the spirit of loving service and sacrifice that was embodied by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself. Make this our personal standard of service to the Vaiṣṇavas.

We beg that you accept our prayers unto your divine lotus feet on this most auspicious occasion, and we humbly request you to continue to give us service unto your lotus feet.

Your insignificant sons and daughters, grandsons and granddaughters, in Śrī Dhāma Māyāpur.

## Melbourne

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

This year, 1998, marks the twenty-fifth anniversary of the installation of our Melbourne temple’s presiding Deities, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Vallabha.

Mid-morning on that sacred Saturday you descended the steep stairs of our former temple in St. Kilda appearing like a grave ancient sage. You entered the packed temple room for the installation ceremony and took your seat adjoining the small brick fire pit.

Madhudviṣa Mahārāja carried in the marble Deities, still covered with pink satin cloth, and placed them one by one into small bathing receptacles in front of you. While Śrutakīrti decorated the sacrificial arena with colored rice flour, Madhudviṣa lifted the cloth that covered Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. When the cloth revealed Her Lordship, the devotees gasped, marveling at Her attractive features. You gazed lovingly at Rādhārāṇī, noting her pure white complexion and fresh youthful beauty.

The *kīrtana* commenced as you personally poured a small brass lota of scented water over Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī’s head, all the while gazing at Rādhārāṇī with a look saturated with love. Next the cloth covering was removed from Kṛṣṇa. Glossy and black in complexion, Kṛṣṇa also accepted your loving service by way of the inaugural bath.

After the installation ceremony, you initiated some devotees and lit the sacrificial fire. The customary *mantras* were intoned to the accompaniment of *kīrtana* and readings from sacred texts. As devotees chanted *Svāhā!* and threw *ghee*-soaked grains into the flames, you poured a steady stream of *ghee* that appeared like liquid gold into the blazing flames that leapt higher and higher.

When the bananas were placed into the fire, thick smoke quickly filled the small terrace-house temple. You reminded us that the smoke was “very nice” and that we shouldn’t open the windows. You rose from your seat and entered the Deity room, the conchshell blew, and open doors revealed the beautiful Deities situated on Their new altar surrounded by billowing plumes of frankincense smoke.

You gracefully offered the articles one by one: first the incense, then the blazing camphor lamp, *ghee* lamp, and scented water, each in large elegant circular movements that appeared to be in slow motion. The silk cloth, the scented white carnations, and the peacock feather fan were offered with similar deep devotion. We were inspired as you gazed at the beautiful forms of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa with a fixed intensity of concentration that left no doubt that you were seeing Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa face to face.

The *kīrtana* continued as you returned to your room and gave your two young newly second-initiated disciples, Ganeśa dāsa and Śukla dāsī, the Gāyatrī *mantra,* reiterating the importance of the sacred connection to the spiritual master. Your instructions to the two young disciples were short but heavy with meaning. “Look after Kṛṣṇa very nicely,” you said in earnest.

You expressed your pleasure that the devotees had made nice arrangements to receive Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in Melbourne. You particularly appreciated the beauty of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī and named the Deities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Vallabha.

A quarter of a century later Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are still kindly receiving our offerings of worship. Your servants here, some old ones and some newly recruited, are still trying to look after Them nicely, keep the temple clean, just as you asked us to, and to also feed “all the hungry men of Melbourne” the *prasādam* of the Deity, just as you asked us to.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have kindly given us Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa to look after, and in return, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are looking after us. Just as the servants of the king enjoy the benefits of royal palace life, Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are sharing Their palatial home, Their daily *darśana,* and Their daily offerings of sumptuous food with their fortunate servants. You have created our good fortune and inspired us to help create the good fortune of others.

On this auspicious day, let us pray as you have done, following in the footsteps of your beloved Prahlāda Mahārāja.

*Svasty astu viśvasya*—may there be good fortune throughout the universe*. Khalaḥ prasīdatām*—may all envious persons be pacified. May all living entities become calm by practicing *bhakti-yoga,* and may we all engage in the service of the Supreme transcendence, being always absorbed in thought of Him.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, despite my lack of qualifications, somehow I’m also still here, still trying to assist you in the service of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in Melbourne, which you described as “the Southernmost part of this globe.” I pray for the chance to stay here, fixed in your service, and I again thank you for not kicking me away, despite my rascaldom.

Your servants at Mahāprabhu Mandir, Melbourne, Australia.  
(written by Kūrma dāsa)

## Mexico City

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, O savior of the fallen, how merciful you are on this most auspicious day of your appearance!

We pray intensely at your lotus feet for the protection of this most sacred place in Mexico, the abode of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-Gopāla. Unfortunately, this most beautiful house that you visited twice and liked so much is in jeopardy due to a lack of organization and devotee unity that have plagued us for years. We are sure that Kṛṣṇa is dissatisfied with us, and that is why we have so many legal problems to secure Kṛṣṇa’s property. Please be kind to us and forgive our madness. Guide us in this herculean task of maintaining a cool mind and taking the proper steps to defeat the demons, who oppose the Lord’s supremacy and want to illegally snatch your property.

On this most sacred day we are very much ashamed not to be able to offer you anything pleasing except our determination to serve you forever and our desire to guard your house even at the cost of our lives. We should be planning to develop this project in a way that projects have been developed in many other countries, thus satisfying you. But instead we are only praying to Your Divine Grace for the strength to remain sheltered at your lotus feet. Please bless us so we can always think properly, only of your pleasure, and always remembering your lotus feet forever. Please bless us with full Kṛṣṇa conscious strength to stand up to any obstacle in the execution of your orders and your will.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, next year we hope to offer you, as a gift for your transcendental pleasure and glory, the news that your house in the biggest city of the world is securely in ISKCON’S name. There it will remain for ages to come, sheltering generation after generation of devotees who will sing the praises of your name, distribute your books, and chant the Holy Name, and your house will also give shelter, without distinction, to the unfortunate souls of this infernal city.

All over the world your Society is growing and maturing, and also facing and overcoming difficulties. Please give us your shelter so we can comprehend your wishes and teachings and work together despite all our difficulties. In this way, by placing into the deepest region of our hearts even your most insignificant desire and making it our life and soul, we may help you fulfill your mission.

We pray for the determination to take the holy name to every town and village, as you desire.

Your servants in the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-Gopāla Mandir in Mexico City.  
(written by Rohiṇīnandana dāsa)

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most blessed anniversary of your appearance day.

You appeared in this world, bringing the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to the fallen conditioned souls. Working day and night—preaching, translating literature, establishing temples and farms—you arranged for books to be printed and distributed. Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, could achieve all this. We cannot measure the magnitude of your kindness, nor can we possibly estimate the great fortune we enjoy by having you as our eternal well-wisher.

You were so kind that you even came to such a place as Miami. You came and established Kṛṣṇa’s mercy here. Now your *mūrti* sits on the *vyāsāsana,* giving us the shelter of your lotus feet and the transcendental inspiration to awaken Kṛṣṇa consciousness in everyone’s heart.

As stated in the *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Antya* 7.11):

*kali-kālera dharma—kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana*

**“The fundamental religious system in the Age of Kali is the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Unless empowered by Kṛṣṇa, one cannot propagate the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **movement.”**

You are the best example of an empowered preacher who pushed on Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. The order of Lord Caitanya is to spread this movement all over of the world, to every town and village. And you have followed the instruction of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, perfectly, fulfilling the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**“In every town and village, My name will be chanted.”**

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants in New Naimiṣāraṇya, Miami, Florida, USA.

## Montreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your holy lotus feet.

As we try to glorify you on this auspicious day of your appearance, our words seem inadequate. We are experiencing daily that this material world is full of suffering. Seeing our sorrowful condition, you came to America, traveling alone at the age of seventy to reclaim the fallen souls back to sanity. Despite the uncomfortable journey on the ship from India, you were determined to carry out your spiritual master’s order to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western countries. Within only twelve short years you opened ISKCON centers in all the major cities of the world. You also kindly translated all the important scriptures and taught us the basics of how to live as human beings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a great *mahā-bhāgavata,* for your books have helped millions of conditioned souls. Only by taking shelter of your instructions can one be saved from the clutches of birth, death, old age, and disease.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have distributed the wealth of *bhakti* without discrimination concerning who should receive it. Please bless us so that we can carry out your instructions in a way that will please you—by distributing the holy name, your transcendental books, and *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*.

We beg for your mercy to free us from envy, anger, and false pride, and to help us overcome all the obstacles hindering our pushing on Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement.

May we, your lowly servants at the Montreal *yātrā,* eternally take shelter of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your loving disciples, grand-disciples, bhaktas, and congregational followers in Montreal, Canada.  
(written by Samir Roy Choudhury)

## Mumbai

Dear Śrīla PrabhupādaPlease accept our obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, which are the only shelter for all those desiring freedom from this material life.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Once you said, “Bombay is my office.” In view of this statement, we feel the grave responsibility given us here in Juhu.

The other day when Girirāj Swami was looking for some papers to help in the BBT court case in America, it took us a while to find them, for they were tucked away in a file cabinet. You had signed these papers some twenty-four years ago. From this we could understand that if your office is not maintained properly, things will not be available. So, we pray to you on this most auspicious day that we may always remain conscious that this center here at Juhu as your office, and we must make sure that it runs efficiently.

Your servants here at Juhu also pray that we may be able to engage every inch of Hare Krishna Land in your service. We know this will be possible when we are no longer self-interested but desire only the pleasure of Your Divine Grace.

Your personal Deities, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rasabihārī, are attracting thousands of people every day. People are coming from all over the world to stand in front of their Lordships and offer their prayers. Everyone who comes sees you sitting on the *vyāsāsana,* and it is wonderful how the people pay their respects to you. Sometimes we can see a competition going on between you and Kṛṣṇa. You are bringing so many people to worship Kṛṣṇa, and Kṛṣṇa is directing all those people to glorify you. We cannot calculate our good fortune to be able to assist you in your service to Guru and Gaurāṅga. We can only pray that somehow, even though we have no qualification, we can continue to serve you and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rasabihārī here at your office in Juhu.

You were always proud of Hare Krishna Land. Once in a class in Geneva you mentioned that you had bought such a nice piece of land at a very good price. You called it Hare Krishna Land and a garden paradise. Today we ask your guidance to make and keep this land as you wanted it, “heaven on earth.”

This year we held Ratha-yātrā and the annual Hare Kṛṣṇa Festival in Andheri, near the temple. We have plans to increase the number of festivals to three. One festival will be downtown, another will be near Juhu, and the third will be on the north side of the city in Mira Road. We want to increase the number of carts to three and also to include floats depicting the pastimes of the Lord in His various incarnations. Our hope is to please you by engaging as many people as possible in the service of the Lord. For as it is mentioned in the prayer by Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura, *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*—the only way to make spiritual advancement is to please the spiritual master.

Without your personal help we would fall victim to sense gratification in a moment. Every devotee here feels connected to you, through reading your books, selling your books, hearing your tapes, visiting your quarters, and so on. In so many ways the devotees feel your loving reciprocation.

Once in the Denver airport a reporter asked you, “What will happen to this movement when you die.” You replied, “I will never die.” All the devotees shouted “Hari bol!” and “Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!” Then you said, “I will live forever in my books.” Some people feel you have left, but for us at Hare Krishna Land you are alive and well. We see your preaching going on every day. Thousands of people are coming to the temple and appreciating the beauty of your Deities, taking *prasādam,* and buying your books. How is it happening? It is because of your presence, and the more we try to understand that, the more the preaching will increase.

Without serving your lotus feet, no one can escape the blazing fire of material existence. So, today we want to thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for taking so much trouble to establish the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rasabihārī Mandir at Hare Krishna Land, Juhu. You have given us a lifetime of your service, and in appreciation of this we pray we may give others the opportunity to serve you.

Your servants at your office, Mumbai, India.

## Murāri-sevaka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and loving obeisances. All glories to you and to your lotus feet!

In this remote area of Tennessee, USA, we have every opportunity to live and practice peaceful village life. Some of the areas we are working in are agriculture, cow protection, alternative energy and healing, music, education, and simple living. We are also learning to love and respect one another. In this way we hope to please you not only on your Vyāsa-pūjā but every day.

There is always so much service to do. Bringing neglected projects up to speed is a tremendous task. Raising the standard of worship for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurachandra requires lots of dedication. Learning to communicate with one another and keep idle village talk to a minimum is our greatest challenge. The wonderful thing is, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we all want to reach the next level in our effort toward accomplishing these goals. Naturally Māyā is testing us every step of the way.

We have to rise above our material defects and set the proper example for the rest of society to follow. There are hundreds of communal groups, but none of them have mastered village gossip, and none of them have the gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Everything we need to know about how to achieve our goals is in your books. We must learn not only to read your teachings but to actually live them! You have given us all we need for a perfect society. You have shown us the way not only to live happily in the material realm but also to go back to the spiritual world.

Our prayer to you in closing:

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most glorious and important day in the universe, please give us the spiritual and material strength to meet the challenges in devotional service. Allow us the intelligence to see beyond our material attachments, to see everything as Kṛṣṇa and His energies. Show us how to turn lust into love. Let us see Kṛṣṇa in our hearts with spiritual eyes tinged with the salve of love and devotion.

Thank you for allowing us to see a glimpse of real life beyond the animalistic depths we were mired in. Thank you for allowing us to serve you through serving Murāri-sevaka. Thank you for allowing us to sing and chant your glories. Thank you for being you. We love you. Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Your disciples and grand-disciples at the Murāri-sevaka farm, Mulberry, Tennessee, USA.  
(written by Drumila dāsa)

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet.

We are fallen and full of innumerable faults and bad qualities, yet still out of your sweet and causeless mercy you have accepted us as your servants and are engaging us in the highest possible activities. We cannot truly comprehend just what you have done for us and the entire human society. But we are hoping that the Lord will help us understand your great compassion upon all of us, so that we can try to glorify you in a way that may be at least a little befitting your most elevated position.

By your causeless mercy, we are very fortunate to be preaching the glories of the holy name in Nagpur. When we meet people and encourage them to visit your temple, they can have the *darśana* of the very beautiful Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopināth. By your grace, we worship and take shelter of Them. The congregation is increasing—we have made many new friends and are working on deepening our relationships with old friends. By your mercy, we find ourselves in ever-new situations, forced to increase our surrender to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

We, your grand-disciples here in Nagpur, are actually ashamed that we have not got much to offer you, much less than what many other *yātrās* are offering you. We are trying to please you by cooperating to push on your movement, and we hope that we are pleasing you in our small way. In the future, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we hope to have a center in Nagpur filled with blossoming Vaiṣṇavas to spread the nectarean aroma of your glories and the holy name.

Therefore, on the auspicious day of your divine appearance, we fervently pray that you kindly bless us so that we can worship you with causeless devotional service. Please help us become mature and useful servants of your wonderful society, ISKCON.

The servants of your servants in Nagpur, India.

## Nava-jiyaḍa-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

All glories to you, who appeared like the sun on the horizon of this world covered by fog and ignorance. Falsely thinking ourselves the body, foolishly thinking that everything is meant for our enjoyment, we wasted our time by gradually sinking to the lower levels of consciousness, constantly experiencing pain and suffering in the clutches of the material energy. We thought we were God, but lived like the dogs. On the edge of existence, there was no hope. And then you came into our lives and saved us from illusion. You opened our eyes and enabled us to see reality with Kṛṣṇa in the center. That’s why we thank you. With pleasure we stay indebted to you, engaged in your service by pushing on the *saṅkīrtana* mission, the supreme engagement. Being under your protection is all that counts.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your plan to spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is majestic. All the different activities of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement create a wonderful transcendental symphony under your direction. Led by your desire, the various instruments play harmoniously for your satisfaction and the satisfaction of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. This music, the powerful creation of Lord Caitanya’s mercy, will spread everywhere, flooding the entire universe, until everyone drowns in pure love of Godhead. And we can see with our own eyes how this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is making a bigger and bigger impact on the world. The transcendental knowledge emanating from the most wonderful books of Your Divine Grace is infiltrating the consciousness of the people. And they are not even aware of it. This we can see clearly when we go out and try to present your books. What ten years ago was completely new and unknown is nowadays the accepted fact. People are becoming more and more aware that their activities create their future, and they are ready to change themselves, so much so that all the demoniac propaganda cannot stop them from becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. Such is the power and glory of yours books, the most valuable gift to humanity. They are meant to spark a revolution in the impious lives of a misguided humanity. People will finally see the necessity of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

And to facilitate such fortunate souls, you created temple and farm communities—spiritual oases in the desert of the material world, where people can learn what Kṛṣṇa consciousness is and how to apply it in their own lives. They can also learn how to give it to others. That is what we are trying to do here on NJNK. We try to reach all different kinds of people by distributing books, *prasādam,* and transcendental sound. We are trying to cultivate people who are interested and organize Bhakti-Vriksha groups.
