of your Lord and Lady,  
who out of love  
have now come as one,  
as the Golden Lord,  
who is crying—  
crying for the plight  
of the fallen souls.  
Wishing an end  
to their suffering,  
He has sent you,  
the ambassador—  
our lord and master.

On this day,  
holiest of days,  
we pray—  
we will no longer resist  
your call.  
Giving up our selfishness  
and pride,  
becoming sincere  
for your service,  
we will take our place  
at your lotus feet.

Your servants at the Śrī Rūpānuga Paramārthika Vidyāpīṭha, Bhaktivedanta Academy, Māyāpur, India.

## Youth and Vedic Education Project

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga! All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to all the devotees!

By your causeless mercy and that of Kṛṣṇa, we have come in contact with and are trying to apply the pure, exalted teachings of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. These teachings have been delivered by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and made practical by your living example.

We, the devotees in Bandar Lampung, owe you an unrepayable debt of gratitude because of the great services you have rendered, Śrīla Prabhupāda, through your books, lectures, disciples, etc.

Most of us devotees in Lampung are high school or university students and youth. This is indeed in accordance with your desires. You said, “Carry your activities to the university campuses, for the younger generation are our future hope.”

We are now struggling to disseminate Kṛṣṇa consciousness among the youth, especially among high school and university students. This is for your pleasure, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and also so that our life may be successful. As you once said, “If you seriously try to spread this message to the youth in their hearts, then you will be successful.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are respected all over the world by professors and intellectuals. It is only fitting that you be given extraordinary honor, because you are the savior of the whole world. Your appearance in this world has genuinely great significance.

Many of the devotees were formerly not good people. Some were even drug addicts, hippies, etc. Many have also come from good families. This simply proves the purity of your teachings, passed down through the disciplic succession.

Although you have left us physically, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you continue to be with us through your books. Reading your books means taking your association.

It was your desire that someone organize programs of *kīrtana* and lectures in schools and educational institutions. We are in the process of planning this program. At the moment we are endeavoring to construct a Kṛṣṇa conscious hostel near our campus. This hostel will be a center for preaching in Lampung, especially for introducing the teachings of your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* which are the practical books for spiritual life.

Whether our plans will succeed all depends on the desire of Guru and Kṛṣṇa, and your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We have resolved to offer something to you. For preaching, we need a place. If possible, that place should be in a strategic location so we can preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to many people. We wish to offer this place to you. We all owe a great debt of gratitude to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because without you, who knows what would have been our fate? Formerly we were voraciously eating anything, not offering our food to Kṛṣṇa, living impure lives, and so on. But after learning the principles of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we were all moved to give up our bad habits. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are really great. You are capable of changing even that which appears impossible to change. This is your greatness, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We who always aspire for your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Jaya Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

The devotees at ISKCON’s Bandar Lampung, Indonesia, Youth and Vedic Education Project.
